Conclusion: 论文定性地回答了 Bakhtiari 等人提出的开放问题：链接函数的单调性对 TS 获得次线性贝叶斯遗憾并非必要，在任意凸（乃至非单调）链接下 TS 仍具有 $\tilde O(d^{9/2}\sqrt n)$ 的遗憾界。其主要技术贡献包括揭示 John 椭球二分法在非单调情形下的失效、建立无信息配置的 $O(d^2)$ 基数上界及其紧性构造，以及一致的信息比-遗憾转换。局限在于得到的 $d^{9/2}$ 依赖劣于单调情形的 $d^{5/2}$，能否在非单调情形保留 $d^{5/2}$ 依赖仍为开放问题。<br><br>【相关性说明】本文为 bandit 凸优化与 Thompson 采样的纯理论工作，与事件相机、事件流表示、脉冲神经网络、UAV 感知或小目标检测等视觉/机器人方向无直接关联。

Abstract: Bakhtiari, Lattimore and Szepesvári (COLT 2025) proved that Thompson sampling (TS) has Bayesian regret $\tilde O(d^{5/2}\sqrt n)$ for bandit convex optimisation with convex \emph{monotone} ridge losses $f(x)=\ell(\ip{x}θ)$, and asked whether monotonicity of the link is necessary. We give a qualitative negative answer. For every prior on $[0,1]$-valued, $1$-Lipschitz convex ridge losses with an arbitrary convex, possibly non-monotone, link, and for any fixed measurable selection of minimisers, exact-posterior TS has Bayesian regret $O\big((d+1)^4\sqrt{dn}\,\log(e+nd\max\{1,\diam K\})\big)=\tilde O(d^{9/2}\sqrt n)$. The monotone proof relies on a single-removal John-ellipsoid dichotomy; we show by an explicit twelve-point configuration that this dichotomy fails for non-monotone links, and replace it by an $O(d^2)$ cardinality bound for ``uninformative'' configurations. The bound uses a Boolean rounding argument: a $0$-$1$ matrix within $1/(4r)$ in max-norm of a rank-$r$ matrix has rank at most $2r-1$. We construct $d(d+1)$ uninformative losses, showing that the cardinality bound is tight up to constants in the large-diameter-to-gap regime, and give a self-contained information-ratio-to-regret transfer that is uniform over fixed measurable selections. Whether the $d^{5/2}$ dependence of the monotone case can be retained remains open.

</details>


### [144] [Importance Weighting for Unlabeled-unlabeled Learning under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10994)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向无标签-无标签（Unlabeled-Unlabeled, UU）学习的分布偏移自适应方法，通过重要性加权在测试分布风险最小化的框架下，利用训练分布的UU数据与少量测试分布的UU数据来学习二分类器，并证明该方法可统一处理PU学习、噪声标签学习等多种监督场景下的分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UU学习框架可从两组具有不同类别先验的无标签数据中训练二分类器，具有高度通用性（涵盖PU学习、噪声标签学习、基于相似度的学习等）。但现有UU学习方法假设训练分布与测试分布的类条件密度（class-conditional densities）一致，而实际中因分布偏移（distribution shift）该假设往往不成立，导致测试性能下降。因此需要一种能在UU框架下处理分布偏移、且不依赖特定偏移类型假设（如协变量偏移）的通用方法，这对其在真实场景（含视觉感知等任务）中的部署具有重要意义。

Method: 所提方法基于重要性加权（importance weighting）：通过训练数据及其估计出的重要性权重来最小化测试风险。作者证明，在UU设定下重要性权重可以被原则性地（principled manner）估计，从而将重要性加权扩展到传统方法无法处理的UU数据。方法仅需训练分布中的UU数据和测试分布中的少量UU数据，且无需对偏移类型（如covariate shift）作任何假设。得益于UU学习框架的通用性，同一框架可统一处理PU学习、噪声标签学习等多种问题下的分布偏移，而现有方法通常针对特定问题进行定制。

Result: 论文在真实世界数据集上进行了实验，验证了所提方法在分布偏移下UU学习的有效性。实验主要证明了方法相较现有针对特定问题定制的方法具有更好的通用性与性能表现。摘要未披露具体数据集名称、评价指标及量化数值。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将重要性加权原则性引入UU学习以应对分布偏移，提供了首个统一的、不依赖偏移类型假设的分布偏移适应框架，并覆盖PU学习、噪声标签学习等多种监督场景。局限性方面：摘要未提供具体数据集与定量结果，方法有效性可能依赖于对重要性权重估计的准确性以及对少量测试分布UU数据的可用性，其在大规模视觉与实时感知任务中的表现尚待验证。

Abstract: Unlabeled-unlabeled (UU) learning allows us to learn a binary classifier from two sets of unlabeled data with different class-priors. It is a general framework because it includes a wide variety of supervised learning such as positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, noisy label learning, and similarity-based learning. Existing UU learning assumes that the test and training distributions have the same class-conditional densities. However, this assumption rarely holds in practice due to distribution shifts. This paper proposes a distribution shift adaptation method for UU learning that uses UU data in the training distribution and a few UU data in the test distribution. The proposed method is based on the importance weighting, which minimizes the test risk by using training data with estimated importance weights. Although existing importance weighting methods cannot handle UU data, we show that it can be done in a principled manner. Thanks to the generality of UU learning, our method can handle various learning problems such as PU and noisy label learning under distribution shift within a single framework while existing methods are usually tailored to a specific problem. Moreover, it does not require any assumption of the shift types such as covariate shift. We experimentally demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method with real-world datasets.

</details>


### [145] [Topological Necessities: Mechanism-Invariant Strategic Subgoals for Cross-Embodiment Goal-Conditioned Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11014)
*Hao Shi,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"拓扑必然性"(topological necessities)作为长时域目标条件强化学习中的高层控制对象：从离线成功轨迹中通过0维和1维同调提取"必经阶段"(unskippable stages，即所有可行路径都必须穿越的分割集)与"环路路线选择"，构建可枚举的门(gate)集合及其证书，并以递归拓扑门层次结构进入决策回路。该对象独立于产生它的执行器，可在冻结后跨执行器零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域目标条件RL采用分层结构：高层模块提出子目标(subgoal)，低层执行器完成。但现有子目标本质上是价值函数或latent action的隐式副产物，与生成它们的执行器强绑定，缺乏可迁移性与可解释性。作者转而研究一类不同的对象——"每条成功路径都必须穿越的不可避免阶段的有序序列"，该对象可从离线轨迹中恢复，却"不属于任何一条具体轨迹"，具有内在的拓扑属性：不可跳过的阶段是分割集(separating set)，自由空间中的环路强制产生路线选择。这一视角使得高层决策结构不再依赖特定执行器。

Method: 核心方法是将"必经性"用代数拓扑刻画。(1) 在成功轨迹上构建"传输加权载体"(transport-weighted carrier)，即在状态空间上由轨迹导出的加权复形/拓扑结构。(2) 分别在0维和1维同调(homology)下读取该结构：0维同调捕获连通分支意义上的分割/必经点集，1维同调捕获自由空间中的环(loop)，对应不可回避的路线选择。(3) 由此得到一个可枚举的gate集合，并附带"shell-level certificates"(证书)，用于证明某个gate是真正的拓扑必经项——即被认证的gate。(4) 被认证的gate以"递归拓扑门层次结构"(recursive topological gate hierarchy)的形式进入决策回路，作为高层控制器指导子目标排序。关键创新在于：门是从离线数据中提取的、具备证书保证的拓扑不变量，而非由策略网络隐式产生。

Result: 在同构(isomorphic)自由空间设定下，该对象可跨执行器替换而存续。具体地：在PointMaze数据上冻结的gate无需重新训练即可迁移到Ant和Humanoid；在统一接口下取得最高的Humanoid聚合分数96.1；在多路线(multi-route)任务上相比地图特权(map-privileged)参考基线高出+36.0(p=1.4e-5)。规划器在PointMaze上饱和(100±0)，在AntMaze(giant +22.9)和Kitchen(+15.8/+12.6)上匹配或超越最强基线。评价指标为聚合成功率/性能分数，实验覆盖PointMaze、AntMaze、Kitchen等多类长时域任务。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出并形式化"拓扑必然性"这一新的高层控制对象，用同调方法从离线轨迹中提取带证书的必经门，并证明其在执行器替换下的迁移能力与在多个长时域基准上的优越性能。其意义在于将分层RL的子目标从执行器绑定的隐式量转变为数据驱动、拓扑可认证、可复用的结构。局限方面：论文强调依赖于"固定的、同构的自由空间"这一前提，跨环境拓扑变化(非同构)时的适用性、以及门提取对离线轨迹覆盖度的敏感性未被充分探讨；此外方法基于同调，对更高维高阶拓扑约束的刻画能力有限。

Abstract: Long-horizon goal-conditioned reinforcement learning delegates control to a high-level module that proposes subgoals, but existing subgoals are implicit byproducts of value functions or latent actions, tied to the executor that produced them. We study a different object: a route-conditioned order of unavoidable stages that every successful executor must traverse, recoverable from offline trajectories and belonging to none of them. Its defining properties are topological: an unskippable stage is a separating set that every admissible path must cross, and a loop in free space forces a route choice. We read the two by homology in dimensions 0 and 1 over a transport-weighted carrier built from successful trajectories, yielding an enumerable gate set with shell-level certificates; the certified gates are what we call topological necessities. Certified gates enter the decision loop as a recursive topological gate hierarchy. Under a fixed, isomorphic free space, the object survives executor replacement: gates frozen on PointMaze data transfer without retraining to Ant and Humanoid, attaining the highest Humanoid aggregate under a unified interface (96.1), with +36.0 over a map-privileged reference on the multi-route task (p=1.4e-5); the planner saturates PointMaze (100+/-0) and matches or exceeds the strongest baselines on AntMaze (giant +22.9) and Kitchen (+15.8/+12.6).

</details>


### [146] [T1: Terminal Agent Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11042)
*Junyao Yang,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Ruhan Wang,Zongxia Li,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 T1，一个总参数量 122B 的 Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模型，通过强化学习训练用于长时程终端（terminal）任务。模型在云沙箱中操作真实 shell，单任务可执行 300+ 轮工具调用，并以各任务自带的 verifier 作为奖励信号。作者给出了一套完整训练配方：warm-start、dense process reward、TITO（精确采样 token 训练）、R3（rollout routing replay）以及完全分布外（OOD）语料。在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上把基座从 43.8% 提升到 64.0%，在 Long-Horizon Terminal Bench 上达到 27.9%，超过 GPT-5.4 与 GLM-5.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体（agent）应用正从短程交互转向编码、科学发现等长时程任务，其中终端任务是关键场景，需要在真实 shell 环境中连续执行大量命令、跨越 300+ 步工具调用，并在多轮交互后仍能保持正确执行与目标一致性。此类任务面临两大挑战：一是长时程 RL 训练中 actor-critic 不稳定、训练与推理分布不一致（log-probability 漂移、token 漂移）导致策略退化；二是基准污染风险——若训练语料与评测基准重叠，性能提升可能只是过拟合而非真实能力迁移。因此如何稳定地训练一个能泛化到未见终端任务的大型 MoE 智能体，是核心问题。

Method: 方法由三部分构成。(1) 训练稳定性：采用激进 warm-start 以稳定 actor-critic 训练，并引入 dense process reward，以轨迹中“通过 verifier 的绝对数量”作为连续打分信号，而非仅依赖稀疏的最终成功奖励。(2) 优化与分布对齐：提出 TITO（Training on the exact sampled Token Identifiers）构造，在采样得到的精确 token 标识上进行训练，并在 turn 边界进行 drift repair；同时提出 R3（Rollout Routing Replay），记录采样器在每一 MoE 层每个 token 上的 expert 选择，并在训练时重放，从而对齐 MoE 路由行为，消除训练-推理不一致。(3) 数据构造：使用完全分布外的训练语料，由隔离 seed 与合成任务组成，与 Terminal-Bench 2.1 不相交，以保证收益反映真实能力迁移。模型总参数量 122B，MoE 架构，在云沙箱中操作真实 shell，单任务最多 300+ 轮工具调用，奖励由任务自带 verifier 执行判定。

Result: 实验在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 与 Long-Horizon Terminal Bench 上进行，评价指标为任务解决率（resolved %）。TITO 与 R3 的结合将训练到推理的 log-probability 差异从 0.021 降至 0.013，并在 loss 区域实现完全对齐、零 token 漂移。经后训练流水线，T1 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上由基座模型的 43.8% 提升至 64.0% resolved；在 Long-Horizon Terminal Bench 上达到 27.9%，超过 GPT-5.4 与 GLM-5.1。训练语料与基准完全不相交，说明提升并非来自基准过拟合。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：面向长时程终端任务构建 122B MoE 智能体 T1；提出 TITO 与 R3 以对齐采样与训练分布、消除 token 与路由漂移；使用 dense process reward 与 OOD 语料保证训练稳定性与能力迁移。该方法在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 与 Long-Horizon Terminal Bench 上取得显著提升并超越多个强基线。局限在于：训练与推理依赖真实 shell 沙箱及任务自带 verifier，成本较高且适用性受限于可被自动验证的任务；结果主要集中于终端/代码类长时程任务，向其他智能体领域的泛化性尚待验证；论文未提供与事件视觉、时序感知或轻量化边缘部署相关的实验，因此与事件相机、SNN 等方向暂无直接关联。

Abstract: Agent usage is shifting toward long-horizon tasks such as coding and scientific discovery, among which terminal tasks are especially important. We introduce T1, a Mixture-of-Experts model of 122B total trained with reinforcement learning, operating a real shell in a cloud sandbox for up to 300+ tool-call turns per task, rewarded by executing each task's own verifier. We provide a comprehensive recipe: First, an aggressively warm-started to stabilize actor-critic training, with a dense process reward scoring trajectories by the absolute number of passing verifiers. Second, stable optimization through TITO construction, training on the exact sampled token identifiers with drift repair at turn boundaries, and rollout routing replay, recording the sampler's per-token expert choices at every MoE layer and replaying them during training. Third, fully out-of-distribution training corpus: isolated seeds and synthesized tasks disjoint from Terminal-Bench 2.1 ensures gains reflect genuine capability transfer over benchmark overfitting. Together, TITO and R3 cut the training-to-inference log-probability difference from 0.021 to 0.013, with exactly aligned zero token drift in the loss region. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, our post-train pipeline raises initial base model from 43.8% to T1 with 64.0% resolved. On Long-Horizon Terminal Bench, T1 reaches 27.9% and surpasses GPT-5.4 and GLM-5.1.

</details>


### [147] [EMMI: Edge Multi-Modal Intelligence for Communication-Efficient MLLM Inference via Fused Representation Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11058)
*Motahare Mounesan,Irfan Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 EMMI（Edge Multi-Modal Intelligence），一种以"紧凑表征"为核心的边缘-服务器协同推理框架：边缘端对多模态传感器数据（视觉、文本、遥测）进行模态特定编码、跨模态融合与学习式压缩，仅将固定大小的紧凑潜在表征上传至服务器端进行大模型推理，从而在带宽受限条件下实现通信高效的边缘 MLLM 部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）为边缘智能带来跨异构传感器模态（视觉、文本、遥测）推理的新可能，但其计算、内存与通信开销巨大，难以直接部署于资源受限的边缘平台。现有方案存在两类典型妥协：一是直接上传原始传感器观测（通信负担大、隐私风险高），二是在中间层对神经网络进行划分（partitioning，对异构设备与网络条件敏感、接口不固定）。论文的动机在于寻找一种既降低通信开销、又保护本地隐私、且能在异构边缘设备与服务器 MLLM 之间提供稳定接口的推理范式。

Method: EMMI 采用"表征中心"（representation-centric）的设计范式，与传统的原始数据传输或中间层网络划分形成对照。其流水线包含三个边缘端关键环节：(1) 模态特定编码（modality-specific encoding），为不同传感器模态分别提取特征；(2) 跨模态表征融合（cross-modal representation fusion），将多模态特征整合为统一表征；(3) 学习式压缩（learned compression），将融合表征进一步压缩为紧凑潜在表示（compact latent representation）。仅该紧凑表征被传至服务器端，由高容量 MLLM 完成推理。该设计带来三个特性：通信开销显著降低、本地原始数据不外传从而保护隐私、边缘设备与服务器 MLLM 之间形成固定大小的表征接口，便于适配异构硬件。

Result: 论文在一个具有代表性的多模态基准上进行评估。结果显示：在保持下游任务精度可比（comparable accuracy）的前提下，EMMI 将通信负载降低约 32 倍（32x）；在带宽受限的边缘条件下，估计的端到端推理时延最高可降低 3.4 倍（3.4x）。上述数据为论文摘要中报告的核心定量结论，摘要未披露具体数据集名称、模态组合细节与评估指标。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出并验证了一种面向边缘 MLLM 推理的表征级通信范式：通过模态编码—跨模态融合—学习式压缩的级联设计，在显著压缩通信负载（32x）的同时维持下游精度，并在带宽受限场景下带来最高 3.4x 的端到端时延收益，同时兼顾隐私与异构设备适配。其局限在于：评估仅基于单一"代表性"多模态基准，泛化性有待更多任务与数据集验证；时延数据为"估计值"（estimated）而非实测部署结果；摘要未说明压缩率与精度之间的权衡曲线、对具体模态（如是否涉及事件相机等高频感知模态）的适用性，以及边缘端编码与压缩本身带来的额外计算开销。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language mod- els (MLLMs) have opened new opportunities for edge intelligence by enabling reasoning across heterogeneous sensor modalities, such as vision, text, and telemetry data. However, deploying these capabilities on resource-constrained edge platforms remains challenging due to the substantial computational, memory, and communication demands of modern MLLMs. Rather than transmitting raw sensor observations or partitioning neural networks at intermediate layers, Edge Multi-Modal Intelligence (EMMI) communicates a compact representation between edge devices and server resources, enabling communication-efficient edge MLLM inference. To achieve this, EMMI performs modality-specific encoding, cross-modal representation fusion, and learned compression at the edge, transmitting only a compact latent representation to server-side resources for high-capacity MLLM reasoning. This representation-centric design reduces communication overhead, preserves local data privacy, and provides a fixed-size interface between heterogeneous edge devices and server-side MLLMs. Evaluation on a representative multimodal benchmark demonstrates that EMMI can reduce the communication payload by 32x while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy, resulting in up to a 3.4x reduction in estimated end-to-end inference latency under bandwidth-constrained edge conditions.

</details>


### [148] [The information geometry of large language models is shared, learned, and controllable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11063)
*Dario Picozzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出以「下一 token 概率的 Fisher-Rao 几何」作为刻画大语言模型行为结构的统一框架。作者论证：行为决定了这一几何（在保持输出不变的对称性意义下唯一），而激活几何依赖坐标系因而不是内在量。基于此，论文展示了跨 Transformer、状态空间（SSM）与循环（RNN）模型的行为几何一致性，并利用该几何推导出最小扰动式的局部干预方法，在 steering、模型编辑、归因、字典学习和微调等任务中优于欧氏控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文面对的核心问题是：不同大语言模型学到的行为高度相似，但这种相似性背后的共同「结构」是什么，以及如何在不破坏其他行为的前提下精确修改某一个行为。现有工作多从激活空间（activation space）分析模型表征，但激活几何依赖于所选的坐标/基，缺乏跨模型可比的不变性，因此难以作为行为的本质刻画。论文希望找到一个内蕴的（intrinsic）几何对象——下一 token 概率分布族上的 Fisher-Rao 度量——来统一回答「模型共享什么结构」与「如何可控地改变行为」这两个问题。这对模型可解释性、模型编辑、对齐与可控生成都有重要意义。

Method: 论文以统计流形视角建模：把模型在每个上下文下输出的下一 token 概率分布视为流形上的点，赋予 Fisher-Rao 度量，从而得到行为层面的黎曼几何。关键论点有二：(1) 该几何在保持输出分布的对称变换（output-preserving symmetries）下是被行为唯一确定的，因此是行为的内在表征；(2) 激活空间几何会随坐标选择变化，故不宜作为跨模型不变量。

Result: 跨架构（Transformer、状态空间、循环模型）的对比显示：输出几何比激活几何的一致性更强，且共享的几何结构支持语义类别的迁移。与人类词选择的吻合度随预测准确率、模型规模与训练程度上升而提高，模型侧的校准（model-only calibration）可进一步提升该吻合度。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出并系统验证了以 Fisher-Rao 几何作为大语言模型行为内在结构的统一框架：它既能解释跨架构的行为相似性与语义迁移，又能解释事实习得的语料统计依赖与证据深度的延迟效应，且该几何可直接指导最小扰动干预，并在多种任务上产生可复用的控制信号。

Abstract: Large language models learn similar behaviours, yet it remains unclear what structure they share or how to change one behaviour without disturbing others. The Fisher-Rao geometry of next-token probabilities connects these questions: behaviour determines this geometry up to output-preserving symmetries, whereas activation geometry depends on coordinates. Across transformer, state-space and recurrent models, output geometries agree more strongly than activation geometries, and shared geometry supports semantic-category transfer. Agreement with human word choices increases with predictive accuracy, scale and training, and improves further after model-only calibration. Token probabilities and read-out geometry jointly predict the spectrum and its effective dimension. Controlled language assignments show that geometry follows the language law across architectures. Pretraining corpus statistics predict held-out fact acquisition without recalibration, while randomised experiments show that deeper evidence substantially delays acquisition across every tested architecture and evidence construction. Finally, the geometry prescribes minimum-disturbance local interventions, predicts their relative cost, and supports reusable control: updates learned on donor prompts transfer to unseen prompts while better preserving behaviour on reference prompts than Euclidean control. The same geometric correction improves steering, editing, attribution, dictionary learning and fine-tuning.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond Solver Verdicts: Generative Reward Models for Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11085)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Aman Goel,Ali Torkamani,Xiaoxue Han,Joseph Lilien,Ferhat Erata,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文形式化定义了神经符号系统中"判定保持的不忠实性"（Verdict-Preserving-Unfaithfulness, VPU）问题，即错误的形式化编码仍能通过求解器得到期望判定结果；理论上证明结构化、仅依赖判定的验证启发式方法在此类欺骗性轨迹上只能达到随机水平；提出生成式验证（GenV），将离线Z3等价性判定器蒸馏为无需参考的连续等价性评分，并给出下游任务增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号（neurosymbolic）系统依赖Z3等数学求解器保证推理正确性，但求解器本身"看不见"形式化翻译是否与指定的形式化描述保持严格参考等价（reference-equivalence）。这意味着一个错误编码（incorrect encoding）可能成功执行并输出与期望一致的判定，从而形成隐蔽的失效模式——VPU。该问题的重要性在于：它使求解器验证的可靠性产生根本漏洞，仅凭"判定是否正确"的验证方式无法识别这类错误，威胁整个神经符号推理流水线的可信度。

Method: 核心方法为生成式验证（GenV）：将离线的Z3等价性判定器（oracle）蒸馏为一个无需参考（reference-free）的连续参考等价性评分，通过复用语言模型原生词表空间（native vocabulary space）来实现该连续分数。机制分析（mechanistic analysis）方面，作者使用决策投影logit透镜（decision-projected logit lenses）和稀疏自编码器（SAE）表明，生成式读出能够原生提取精确的空间错误坐标，而无需显式定位训练。此外提出GenV+HN（oracle-mined verifier）作为具体验证器实现。理论部分证明了结构化、仅判定式验证启发式在欺骗性有效轨迹上被数学限定为随机水平检测。

Result: 实验方面，oracle-mined验证器GenV+HN在参考等价性验证任务中取得0.961 AUROC；在未见过的翻译器（unseen translators）和不同形式化风格（divergent formal styles）上实现零样本泛化；在智能体测试时计算分配（agentic test-time compute allocation）任务中带来11.3个百分点的下游准确率提升。评测指标包括AUROC、零样本泛化能力与下游准确率。理论结果证明结构性判定验证方法的检测能力上限为随机水平。

Conclusion: 论文主要贡献包括：（1）形式化并理论刻画了VPU这一神经符号系统的隐蔽失效模式，证明判定式验证启发式的数学局限；（2）提出GenV，通过复用语言模型词表空间将Z3等价性判定蒸馏为无参考连续等价性评分；（3）通过机制可解释性分析展示其原生定位错误坐标的能力；（4）在等价性验证与下游测试时计算分配上取得显著增益并展现零样本泛化。局限方面（基于摘要推断）：方法依赖离线Z3等价性判定器作为蒸馏监督信号，其适用性可能受限于可形式化为Z3问题的推理领域。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems rely on mathematical solvers to guarantee reasoning correctness, yet solvers are fundamentally blind to whether a formal translation maintains strict reference-equivalence to a designated formalization. We formalize this vulnerability as Verdict-Preserving-Unfaithfulness (VPU): a failure mode where an incorrect encoding executes successfully and matches the expected verdict. We theoretically prove that structural, verdict-only verification heuristics are mathematically bounded to chance-level detection on these deceptively valid traces. To resolve this, we introduce Generative Verification (GenV), which distills an offline Z3-equivalence oracle into a reference-free, continuous reference-equivalence score by repurposing the language model's native vocabulary space. Mechanistic analysis via decision-projected logit lenses and sparse autoencoders shows this generative readout natively extracts precise spatial error coordinates without explicit localization training. Empirically, our oracle-mined verifier (GenV+HN) achieves 0.961 AUROC in reference-equivalence verification, generalizes zero-shot across unseen translators and divergent formal styles, and yields an 11.3-point downstream accuracy gain in agentic test-time compute allocation.

</details>


### [150] [HERALD: High-Fidelity Exemplar Retrieval with Adaptive Landmark Distillation for Heterophily-Aware Graph Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11123)
*Sujan Chakraborty,Priyanka Saha,Saptarshi Bej*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERALD 是一种无需梯度的图压缩（graph condensation）框架，通过依据图的异质性（heterophily）比例自适应地调整节点评分与特征选择，在相同存储预算下生成小规模代理图，在异质图上匹配或超越现有最优压缩器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图压缩旨在用一个小的代理图保留原始大图上的下游节点分类性能。现有方法主要依赖 Weisfeiler-Lehman 邻域聚合或基于梯度的分布匹配，二者都隐含假设相邻节点具有相同标签——这一假设在异质图（heterophily，即相邻节点类别往往不同）下失效，导致压缩质量下降。因此需要一个能适应异质性的压缩方法。

Method: HERALD（High-fidelity Exemplar Retrieval with Adaptive Landmark Distillation）为无梯度图压缩框架，核心是将压缩流程中的节点评分与特征选择按测得的异质性比例进行自适应调整：（1）特征选择采用 Fisher 判别性与激活密度联合准则，在异质图上对聚合表示降权；（2）节点评分由原型代表性、决策边界接近度与局部内在维度（LID）加权组合而成，权重由异质性比例驱动的平滑 sigmoid 函数控制；（3）通过按分数排序的 BFS 扩展、Personalised PageRank 剪枝与类别再平衡组装成压缩子图。存储预算与 BONSAI 保持一致以便直接对比。

Result: 在八个涵盖同质与异质设置的基准数据集上进行实验，并在四种 GNN 架构下评估节点分类性能。结果显示 HERALD 在异质图上匹配或超越当前最优压缩器，在同质图上保持竞争力；由于存储预算与 BONSAI 相同，比较具有可比性。

Conclusion: HERALD 的主要贡献是提出一种无梯度、异质性自适应的图压缩框架，通过 Fisher 判别性/激活密度特征选择与融合原型、决策边界与 LID 的自适应节点评分，缓解了传统邻域聚合与梯度匹配在异质图上的假设失效问题。局限在于：摘要未给出具体精度数值与压缩率细粒度分析，且方法主要面向节点分类任务，对其他图任务与更大规模图的可扩展性尚待验证。

Abstract: Graph condensation aims to produce a small surrogate graph that preserves the downstream node-classification performance of a much larger original graph. Existing methods rely on Weisfeiler-Lehman neighbourhood aggregation or gradient-based distribution matching, both of which assume that adjacent nodes share the same label, an assumption that breaks down under heterophily. We propose HERALD (High-fidelity Exemplar Retrieval with Adaptive Landmark Distillation), a gradient-free graph condensation framework that adapts the node scoring and feature selection in the condensation pipeline to the graph's measured heterophily. HERALD selects features via a joint Fisher-discriminability and activation-density criterion that down-weights aggregated representations on heterophilic graphs, and scores nodes by a weighted combination of prototype representativeness, decision-boundary proximity, and Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID), where the weights are driven by a smooth sigmoid function of the heterophily ratio. Nodes are then assembled into a condensed subgraph through score-ordered BFS expansion, Personalised PageRank pruning, and class rebalancing, all at an identical storage budget to BONSAI, enabling direct comparison. Experiments on eight benchmark datasets spanning homophilic and heterophilic settings show that HERALD matches or outperforms state-of-the-art condensers on heterophilic graphs and remains competitive on homophilic ones across four GNN architectures.

</details>


### [151] [How Wrong Can a Good Predictor Be? Diverging Updates with Vanishing Predictive KL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11132)
*Qifu Wen,Shuaijun Liu,Zihan Zhou,Xi Zeng,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上证明：在平稳对称高斯HMM中，近似滤波器的"内部更新映射"与精确贝叶斯更新之间可以存在无界差距，但其预测分布（categorical预测KL）对所有有限K≥2仍可趋向于零。即"内部分歧"并不必然导致"预测失败"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在序列建模与近似贝叶斯推断中，常用滤波器的"更新算子"（belief update）与精确贝叶斯更新之间的分歧来衡量近似质量。人们直觉上认为：若内部belief更新差距大，则预测必然差。论文旨在检验这一直觉是否成立——即内部更新映射的分歧与预测性能损失之间是否存在必然联系，从而厘清"何时压缩/近似滤波器是安全的"这一根本问题。

Method: 构造了一个平稳对称高斯HMM（固定有限K≥2）。在该模型中，精确贝叶斯混合与一个显式的确定性径向滤波器（radial filter）作用于相同的K−1个belief坐标（中心化logits）。作者分析当q→0+时的分离度：在最坏情况下，两者中心化logits的分离度至少随自然置信度尺度L_K(q)线性增长，而二者之间的categorical KL（D_KL(exact‖radial)）在同一见证点（witness）处消失。进一步沿平稳HMM轨迹分析：无切换（no-switch）的典型块会把两个滤波器同时推入共同的"置信锥"（confidence cone），在该区域内softmax曲率会抑制二者的分歧；单个高斯最大事件则控制自适应噪声。利用期望终端KL随H(q)=⌈−log(q)/c⌉+1收敛到零来刻画预测代价。

Result: 进行了等间距高斯扫描实验，覆盖K∈{2,4,8}，展示内部更新差距与预测KL之间的相反趋势；并在长时域上做二值对照实验（binary controls），比较饱和型（saturating）与非饱和型（nonsaturating）递归的差异。主要发现：预测KL随q→0+而消失，与内部更新差距的无界增长并存，验证了理论预言。结果显示，将内部更新差距转化为预测代价需要两个缺失环节：(1) 分离状态对期望损失的贡献；(2) 解码器敏感度。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是从理论上证明：即使内部更新映射与精确贝叶斯更新之间存在无界差距，也不能单凭此判定预测失败，从而否定了"内部更新差距等价于预测质量下降"的朴素假设，并指出内部差距与预测代价之间的两个关键缺失环节。局限性：构造固定于特定K，且未给出"压缩何时无害"的通用判据，也未刻画内部差距何时必然带来任务损失。该工作属于序列推断/近似贝叶斯理论范畴，与事件相机、稀疏/时序感知等应用无直接关联。

Abstract: Accurate posterior prediction need not require accurate approximation of Bayesian updates. We prove that an unbounded gap between the update maps can coexist with vanishing predictive KL for every fixed finite $K\ge2$ in a stationary symmetric Gaussian HMM. Exact Bayesian mixing and an explicit deterministic radial filter act on the same $K-1$ belief coordinates. As $q\to0^+$, their separation in centered logits in the worst case grows at least linearly in the natural confidence scale $L_K(q)$, while their categorical $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\mathrm{exact}\|\mathrm{radial})$ vanishes at the same explicit witness. Along stationary HMM trajectories, the expected terminal KL between filtered posteriors also converges to zero at $H(q)=\lceil-\log(q)/c\rceil+1$. Typical blocks without switches drive both filters into a common confidence cone, where softmax curvature suppresses their disagreement; a single Gaussian maximal event controls adaptive noise. A sweep with equally spaced Gaussians over $K\in\{2,4,8\}$ illustrates the opposing trends, and binary controls at long horizons compare saturating and nonsaturating recurrences. The result isolates two missing links between internal update gaps and predictive cost: the contribution of separating states to expected loss and decoder sensitivity. Thus even an unbounded internal update gap does not by itself certify predictive failure. The construction is fixed in $K$ and does not provide a universal criterion for when compression is harmless or characterize when internal gaps must incur task loss.

</details>


### [152] [Phase-Decoupled, Model-Calibrated Power Control for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11133)
*Jae Gon Kim,Donghoon Yoo,Hanyul Ryu,Sungho Ha,Juyeon Lee,Soojung Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向LLM推理服务的"相位解耦、模型标定"功耗控制策略：将预填充（prefill）与解码（decode）分离部署的两条GPU通道分别施加不同功耗策略——prefill通道用带延迟保证下限的SM时钟窗口，decode通道用自动标定、恰好置于吞吐/延迟悬崖之上的功耗上限。在8×B200节点上服务Qwen3-Coder-480B（FP8）与Qwen3-235B-A22B（NVFP4）MoE模型时，相比NVIDIA Max-Q配置实现能效与延迟的双重Pareto改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心GPU功耗已成为LLM服务容量的核心瓶颈，且生产部署已转向prefill/decode（PD）解耦架构。作者在解耦B200系统上实测NVIDIA的Max-Q推理配置，发现其收益有限（+8.6% tokens/J）、依赖具体模型，并伴随平均端到端延迟代价（+5.2%）——这一代价在仅看吞吐的评测中无法体现。更关键的是，Max-Q将同一套设置同时施加于硬件工作状态截然相反的prefill与decode GPU。作者由此提出假设：最优功耗设置是"已部署的（模型、量化、推理引擎、硬件）组合"的属性，而非GPU型号的属性；两条通道应各自拥有独立配置；要把SLO余量安全地转化为能耗节省，需要运行时SLO守护下的延迟门控标定，而非固定配方。

Method: 提出相位解耦的模型标定控制器：prefill通道运行于SM时钟窗口之下，其时钟下限由构造方式直接充当延迟保证；decode通道则施加功耗上限，该上限由自动标定程序放置在实测到的吞吐/延迟悬崖之上。关键洞察在于，解耦后的decode通道呈平坦的内存受限功耗特征，因此该上限会持续生效（binding），使得POLCA工作中因担心响应式超调而放弃功耗封顶的弱点在此场景下不再存在，同时GPU自身的功耗管理器仍能在封顶下维持吞吐。方法包含自动标定流程与运行时SLO守护机制，并配有decode执行器A/B对比（标定封顶 vs. 静态时钟锁定）。

Result: 在8×B200节点上服务Qwen3-Coder-480B（FP8）、采用agentic负载评测：balanced模式取得+20.4% tokens/J，平均端到端延迟仅+3.5%；相比之下Max-Q为+8.6% tokens/J、延迟+5.2%，两轴均为Pareto改进。在Qwen3-235B-A22B（NVFP4）上，本文方法的每种运行模式在每次重复测试中均满足ITL-p99 SLO，而两个厂商配置均未达标。decode执行器A/B实验显示，标定后的功耗上限优于静态时钟锁定；连续三天运行节省了一对通道32.3%的电量。两项被测模型均为MoE；对稠密模型收益约低5倍，故作者将结论限定于MoE服务场景。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：将LLM服务的能效优化重新定义为与具体（模型、量化、引擎、硬件）绑定的分相位标定问题；给出可落地且带延迟/SLO保证的prefill时钟窗口与decode功耗封顶控制器；并在真实规模MoE服务负载上验证了能效-延迟的双重改进与长期节电效果。意义在于挑战了"统一功耗profile"（如Max-Q）的做法，并揭示了纯吞吐评测所掩盖的延迟代价。局限包括：结论明确限定于MoE模型（稠密模型收益约低5倍）、依赖单一硬件平台（8×B200）与两个模型/量化组合，标定需在实测吞吐/延迟悬崖附近进行，部署时可迁移性尚需更广泛验证。

Abstract: Datacenter GPU power is the binding constraint on LLM serving capacity, and production serving has shifted to prefill/decode (PD) disaggregation. Deploying NVIDIA's Max-Q inference profile on a disaggregated B200 system, we found its realized gain modest (+8.6% tokens/J), model-dependent, and carrying a mean end-to-end latency cost (+5.2%) that throughput-only evaluation does not surface; the profile also applies one setting to prefill and decode GPUs that operate in opposite hardware regimes. We hypothesize that the optimal power setting is a property of the deployed (model, quantization, engine, hardware) combination rather than of the GPU class, that each lane warrants its own profile, and that converting SLO headroom into energy safely requires latency-gated calibration under a runtime SLO guard rather than a fixed recipe. We present a phase-decoupled, model-calibrated controller: the prefill lane runs under an SM-clock window whose floor is a latency guarantee by construction, and the decode lane under a power cap placed by automatic calibration just above a measured throughput/latency cliff. Because a disaggregated decode lane draws flat, memory-bound power, the cap binds continuously, the reactive-overshoot weakness that led POLCA to reject capping is absent, and the GPU's own power manager retains throughput under the cap. On an 8x B200 node serving Qwen3-Coder-480B (FP8) under agentic load, our balanced mode delivers +20.4% tokens/J at +3.5% mean e2e versus +8.6% at +5.2% for Max-Q, a Pareto improvement on both axes. On Qwen3-235B-A22B (NVFP4) every operating mode meets the ITL-p99 SLO in every repetition; both vendor profiles miss it. A decode-actuator A/B shows the calibrated cap beats static clock locks, and a three-day sustained run saves 32.3% of a lane pair's electricity. Both models are MoE; a dense model recovers roughly 5x less, so we scope our claims to MoE serving.

</details>


### [153] [Bidirectional Multimodal Fusion of Sky Images and Time-Series for Solar Forecasting with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11135)
*Ken Chen,Maneesha Perera,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Hansani Weeratunge,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SolCloudLLM 是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的多模态短时光伏功率与全局水平辐照度（GHI）预测框架，通过将天空图像 patch 与数值时间序列 patch 对齐并进行双向多模态融合，统一映射到 LLM 嵌入空间，在 SIRTA 与 SKIPP'D 数据集上取得最高 25.4% 的相对 MSE 降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短时（数十分钟至数小时）光伏功率与 GHI 预测对电网调度、备用容量安排与运行至关重要。该时间尺度上的预测误差主要来自云引起的功率爬坡（cloud-induced ramps）：仅依赖历史数值数据难以预判云的到来，因此地基天空图像成为关键的互补物理信号。同时，预测性能对站点与局部观测条件高度敏感，需要大量站点特定数据，而这类数据往往稀缺。尽管 LLM 已在时间序列预测中展现出竞争力与高数据效率，现有 LLM 预测方法仍以单模态（历史数值序列）为主，如何有效将天空图像融入 LLM 预测框架仍属未被充分探索的开放问题。

Method: 提出 SolCloudLLM，一个 LLM-based 多模态预测框架。核心设计包括：（1）将天空图像切分为 image patches，与时间序列切分为 time-series patches，并在表示层面进行对齐；（2）通过双向多模态融合（bidirectional multimodal fusion）整合两类 patch 的对应表示，得到统一表示；（3）将融合后的统一表示映射进 LLM 的嵌入空间，由 LLM 完成预测。该框架把视觉物理信号（云）与历史数值信号在统一的 patch 化表示中联合建模，而非简单拼接或单模态输入。

Result: 在 SIRTA 与 SKIPP'D 两个数据集上进行大量实验。主要指标为 MSE。结果显示：SolCloudLLM 在所有预测时域上均持续优于最佳基线方法，最大相对 MSE 降低达 25.4%。分层分析（stratified analysis）表明，多模态融合带来的增益主要集中在多云（cloudy）条件下，与"云致爬坡是主要误差来源"的动机一致。在几乎所有的 few-shot 设置下，SolCloudLLM 也表现最佳；而其他深度学习基线在少样本下性能显著下降，甚至常被非学习的物理方法超越。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：首次系统性探索将天空图像引入 LLM-based 多模态光伏预测，提出 patch 对齐 + 双向融合 + LLM 嵌入映射的统一框架，并验证多模态融合在云端场景与数据稀缺场景下的显著优势。意义：为短时 PV/GHI 预测提供了物理信号与数值信号互补的多模态范式，并利用 LLM 的数据效率缓解站点特定数据稀缺问题。局限：摘要未说明模型规模、推理延迟与实时性表现，也未明确评估其他指标（如 MAE、爬坡事件检测能力）；分层分析仅指出增益集中于多云条件，未量化各云况下的具体收益；框架依赖天空图像可用性，在多站点泛化与部署成本方面仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Short-term photovoltaic (PV) power and global horizontal irradiance (GHI) forecasts are essential for effective dispatch, reserve scheduling, and grid operations. At these forecasting horizons, errors are predominantly driven by cloud induced ramps: relying solely on historical numerical data may struggle to anticipate an incoming cloud, making ground-based sky images a crucial complementary physical signal. Furthermore, forecast performance is highly sensitive to location and local observing conditions, creating a strong need for site-specific data that are often scarce. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated competitive performance and high data efficiency in time-series forecasting. Despite their success, existing LLM-based forecasting methods remain predominantly unimodal, relying primarily on historical numerical time-series data. Effectively incorporating sky imagery into an LLM-based forecasting framework remains under-explored and an open challenge. In this paper, we propose SolCloudLLM, an LLM-based multimodal forecasting framework. SolCloudLLM aligns sky-image patches with time-series patches and fuses their corresponding representations through bidirectional multimodal fusion, yielding a unified representation that is subsequently mapped into the embedding space of an LLM. Extensive experiments on the SIRTA and SKIPP'D datasets demonstrate that SolCloudLLM consistently outperforms the best baseline methods in MSE across all forecasting horizons, achieving a maximum relative MSE reduction of 25.4%. Stratified analysis further indicates that the benefits of multimodal fusion are concentrated primarily under cloudy conditions. Notably, SolCloudLLM achieves the best performance in nearly all few-shot settings, whereas other deep learning baselines experience substantial performance degradation and are frequently outperformed by the non-learning physical method.

</details>


### [154] [LILA: Calibration-Free Structured Pruning of Large Language Models via Latent Spectral Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11163)
*Sankar Behera,Dhruv Singh,Anshika Agnihotri,Raj Kumar Choudhary,Satyadev Ahlawat,Yamuna Prasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LILA（Latent-Informed Layer Analysis）提出一种基于谱分布的无训练、无标定数据结构化剪枝准则：通过计算完整 FFN 权重矩阵与被消融神经元后权重矩阵经验奇异值分布之间的 Kolmogorov–Smirnov（KS）距离来评估神经元重要性，在零样本与 LoRA 微调后均达到或超越需要标定/强化学习策略的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化剪枝可实现硬件友好的 LLM 压缩，但现有方法在剪枝时需要标定数据（如 SliceGPT 依赖 WikiText-2）、梯度计算，或训练大型辅助策略网络（如 PruneNet 的 45M 参数 RL 策略）。这些依赖增加了计算与数据成本，且在大模型/数据不可得场景下受限。论文旨在提出一种无需训练、无需标定数据、无需辅助网络的闭式剪枝准则，同时保持原架构不变。

Method: 核心思想是将神经元重要性建模为消融前后权重矩阵谱分布的差异：对 FFN 权重矩阵 W 计算其经验奇异值分布，与消融某个神经元后的 W' 的经验奇异值分布做 KS 距离，据此打分。该准则为闭式谱规则（spectral rule），不依赖梯度、标定数据或辅助策略网络，且剪枝后保留原始架构（不改变层结构）。此外，论文将 LILA 扩展为利用 KS 分数动态分配各层稀疏度预算（非均匀稀疏），并辅以 Neural Tangent Kernel（NTK）理论分析，量化剪枝造成的功能扰动。

Result: 在零样本设定下，LLaMA-2-7B 上 25% 稀疏度时 LILA 比 PruneNet（45M 参数 RL 策略）高 1.57 个百分点；在所有稀疏度下比经 WikiText-2 标定的 SliceGPT 最多高 6.0 个百分点，同时保持原架构。经过一个 epoch 的 LoRA 恢复微调后，LILA（零标定数据）在 LLaMA-2-7B 和 Phi-2 上与高度标定的 SliceGPT 基线差距在 0.48 个百分点内。NTK 分析表明相较随机剪枝，功能失真降低 22 倍。扩展到动态稀疏预算分配后，在中度压缩下取得当前最优的生成质量保持，但更高压缩率下暴露出单层架构的根本瓶颈。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：(1) 提出基于 KS 谱距离的闭式神经元重要性准则，实现无训练、无标定、无辅助网络的结构化剪枝，并保留原架构；(2) 零样本性能超越 RL 策略剪枝与标定剪枝基线，LoRA 恢复后与重度标定基线相当；(3) 通过 NTK 分析为谱重要性准则提供理论支撑（功能失真降低 22 倍）；(4) 推广为基于 KS 分数的动态稀疏分配，在中度压缩下最优。局限：在高压缩率下受限于单层架构瓶颈，性能提升有限，表明单纯依赖单层谱差异难以支撑极端压缩。

Abstract: Structured pruning of large language models (LLMs) offers hardware-efficient compression, yet existing methods require calibration data, gradient computation, or large auxiliary policy networks at pruning time. LILA (\emph{Latent-Informed Layer Analysis}) scores neuron importance via the Kolmogorov--Smirnov (KS) distance between empirical singular value distributions of the full and neuron-ablated feed-forward network (FFN) weight matrix, providing a closed-form spectral rule requiring no training, calibration data, or auxiliary network. Without any fine-tuning, LILA surpasses PruneNet (45M-parameter RL policy) by 1.57~pp in zero-shot accuracy on LLaMA-2-7B at 25\% sparsity, and outperforms WikiText-2-calibrated SliceGPT by up to 6.0~pp across all sparsity levels, while preserving the original architecture. After one epoch of LoRA recovery fine-tuning, LILA achieves highly competitive performance, matching the heavily calibrated SliceGPT baseline to within a 0.48~pp margin across LLaMA-2-7B and Phi-2, despite using zero calibration data. A Neural Tangent Kernel analysis confirms a 22$\times$ reduction in functional distortion versus random pruning, providing theoretical grounding for the spectral importance criterion. Finally, extending LILA to dynamically allocate sparsity budgets via KS-scores yields state-of-the-art generative preservation at moderate compression, while uncovering fundamental single-layer architectural bottlenecks at higher compression regimes.

</details>


### [155] [When does a spectral prior help graph learning? Connectivity-loss estimation under road-network disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11166)
*Van-Truong Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用图神经网络（GNN）学习一个"有界修正项"，对一阶 Fiedler 特征值灵敏度进行残差校正，从而快速估计多边删除后的相对代数连通度损失，用于路网连通性筛查。在合成图与 13 个 OSM 区域（六国）上验证，残差 GCN/GraphSAGE 在空间聚类与边介数定向失效下优于解析基线，且具零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多同步发生的道路链路中断（如灾害、多点故障）需要快速评估其对网络连通性的影响。精确谱重算（如多边删除后重新计算 Fiedler 值/代数连通度）计算代价高，难以大规模实时使用；而纯局部近似（如一阶扰动）在多边同时失效时误差较大。代数连通度是刻画网络鲁棒性的关键谱指标，因此需要在"精确但昂贵"与"局部但粗糙"之间取得实用折中，支撑结构连通性筛查。

Method: 方法将相对代数连通度损失分解为"一阶 Fiedler 灵敏度先验 + GNN 学习的有界残差修正"。输入为图结构信息，输出为对标量损失的校正量，通过有界输出抑制外推风险。比较三种骨干：GCN、GraphSAGE 与边感知 MPNN，并与解析基线（一阶、二阶扰动）对比。失效模式覆盖独立失效、空间聚类失效和边介数定向攻击三类；采用图不相交的合成划分，并进行对 13 个 OSM 区域（六国）的零样本迁移。分析中还考察修正斜率随目标化迁移的变化（残差收缩现象），以及稀疏图的规模扩展性。

Result: 在扩展 OSM 测试中，残差 GCN 将空间失效 MAE 改善 0.0391（95% 分层区间 0.0151–0.0662），残差 GraphSAGE 将定向失效 MAE 改善 0.0257（0.0095–0.0446）。二阶扰动相较一阶仅改善 0.0028–0.0053，说明谱残差的收益主要来自学习的修正而非更高阶解析展开。校正斜率在目标化迁移下下降，表明残差收缩与系统性先验误差相关。留一国（leave-one-country-out）的 OSM-to-OSM 迁移结果混合：残差 GCN 在定向失效上 MAE 改善 0.0622（0.0169–0.1153），但空间失效的点估计变差。稀疏缩放验证扩展至 20,000 节点，并区分一次性谱计算开销与摊销后的筛查成本。

Conclusion: 论文将谱残差建模为结构性连通性筛查中"有用但领域敏感"的归纳偏置，提出用 GNN 学习有界修正以折中精确谱重算与局部近似，并在多类失效模式与跨区域迁移上量化其增益与局限。主要贡献在于残差框架、三种失效场景的系统对比与跨 13 个 OSM 区域的零样本评估；局限在于跨国家迁移收益不稳定（空间失效点估计可能退化）、增益依赖领域与失效类型，且残差收缩提示先验系统性误差对学习修正的影响。需注意该工作面向路网结构连通性筛查，非视觉/事件相机或小目标感知任务。

Abstract: Rapid evaluation of many simultaneous road-link disruptions requires a practical compromise between exact spectral recomputation and local approximation. We estimate relative algebraic-connectivity loss after multi-edge deletion using graph neural networks (GNNs) that learn a bounded correction to a first-order Fiedler sensitivity. The study considers independent, spatially clustered, and edge-betweenness-targeted failures, with graph-disjoint synthetic splits and zero-shot transfer to 13 OpenStreetMap (OSM) areas in six countries. GCN, GraphSAGE, and edge-aware MPNN backbones are compared with analytical baselines. In expanded OSM tests, residual GCN improves spatial-failure MAE by 0.0391 (95% hierarchical interval 0.0151-0.0662), while residual GraphSAGE improves targeted-failure MAE by 0.0257 (0.0095-0.0446). Second-order perturbation improves first-order MAE by only 0.0028-0.0053. Correction slopes decrease under targeted transfer, indicating residual shrinkage around systematic prior error. Leave-one-country-out OSM-to-OSM transfer is mixed: residual GCN improves targeted-failure MAE by 0.0622 (0.0169-0.1153) but worsens the spatial point estimate. Sparse scaling extends to 20,000 nodes and separates one-time spectral setup from amortized screening cost. These results characterize the spectral residual as a useful but domain-sensitive inductive bias for structural connectivity screening. Code, cached networks, and reproducibility artifacts are archived at doi:10.5281/zenodo.22307723.

</details>


### [156] [Semi-Tensor Product-Based Multi-Term Randomized T-SVD and Its Visual Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11168)
*Xingchen Xiao,Feng Zhang,Wenjin Qin,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向三阶张量的半张量积（semi-tensor product）新构造，在任意可逆线性变换诱导的 t-product 框架下打破标准 t-product 的严格维度匹配约束，并据此提出多项半张量积奇异值分解（MSTP-SVD）及其随机化加速版本（MRSTP-SVD），在图像/视频压缩与补全任务上取得更优的低秩逼近精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T-SVD 基于 t-product 已成为处理彩色图像、视频等高维视觉数据的强大工具，但标准 t-product 要求严格的维度兼容（维数匹配）约束，限制了其应用范围。已有基于半张量积（STP）的扩展虽放松了该限制，却仍为单项（single-term）形式，逼近精度有限；同时这些确定性方法在处理大规模张量数据时计算开销高。因此需要一种既保持 T-SVD 闭式性质、又具备更高逼近精度与更低计算成本的方法。

Method: 1) 在任意可逆线性变换诱导的 t-product 框架下，构造一种新的三阶张量半张量积，突破标准 t-product 的刚性维度匹配要求，同时保留 T-SVD 的闭式（closed-form）性质。2) 基于该构造提出多项半张量积奇异值分解（MSTP-SVD），通过整合多个正交分解项来提升低秩逼近精度，克服单项方案的精度不足。3) 为降低多项建模的计算成本，引入随机投影（randomized projection）与幂迭代（power iteration）技术，形成加速的多项随机半张量积 SVD（MRSTP-SVD）算法，在重构精度与计算效率之间取得平衡。

Result: 论文在图像与视频的压缩和补全任务上进行了实验验证，结果表明所提方法有效。摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评价指标与定量数值，仅强调 MSTP-SVD 相较单项方案显著提升了低秩逼近精度，且 MRSTP-SVD 在精度与效率之间实现了平衡。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提出突破维度匹配约束且保持闭式性质的三阶张量半张量积构造；提出提升逼近精度的多项 MSTP-SVD；以及通过随机投影与幂迭代实现加速的 MRSTP-SVD，并在图像/视频压缩与补全任务上验证有效性。局限在于摘要未提供定量实验结果与具体数据集/指标，对大规模张量数据的实际效率提升幅度及与现有方法的全面对比仍有待正文进一步说明。

Abstract: Tensor singular value decomposition (T-SVD), which is built upon the tensor-tensor product (t-product), has emerged as a powerful tool for processing high-dimensional visual data such as color images and videos. However, the standard t-product imposes strict dimensional compatibility constraints. Although extensions based on the semi-tensor product (STP) relax this restriction, their single-term formulations still suffer from limited approximation accuracy. Moreover, these deterministic methods incur high computational costs when processing large-scale tensor data. To address these issues, this paper introduces a novel semi-tensor product for third-order tensors under the t-product framework induced by arbitrary invertible linear transforms. The resulting tensor semi-tensor product breaks the rigid dimension matching requirement of the standard t-product, while retaining the closed-form property of T-SVD. Based on this construction, we develop a multi-term semi-tensor product singular value decomposition (MSTP-SVD), which integrates multiple orthogonal decomposition terms to significantly improve low-rank approximation accuracy compared with single-term schemes. To reduce the computational cost of multi-term modeling, we incorporate randomized projection and power iteration techniques into the MSTP-SVD framework, yielding an accelerated multi-term randomized semi-tensor product SVD (MRSTP-SVD) algorithm that achieves a balance between reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency. Experiments on image and video compression and completion tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [157] [Hierarchical Clustering Can Jointly Satisfy Richness, Consistency, and Scale Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11173)
*Daichi Kuroda,Maximilien Dreveton,Matthias Grossglauser,Patrick Thiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究聚类理论中的一个核心问题：Kleinberg 不可能性定理表明扁平聚类无法同时满足尺度不变性、丰富性和一致性三条公理，作者转而追问当输出为层次结构而非单一划分时，这一不可能性是否仍然成立，并证明层次聚类情况下这些公理可以同时满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类缺乏普遍接受的"簇"定义，Kleinberg 的不可能性定理形式化了这一困难：任何扁平聚类方法都无法同时满足尺度不变性（scale invariance）、丰富性（richness）和一致性（consistency）三条自然公理。该结果常被视为聚类缺乏公理化基础的证据。作者提出问题：如果聚类输出是层次结构（hierarchy）而非单一划分（flat partition），这种不可能性是否依然存在？这一问题关乎层次聚类方法的理论基础与公理化刻画，具有重要的理论意义。

Method: 作者将扁平聚类的三条公理（尺度不变性、丰富性、一致性）推广到层次聚类设定，并证明其层次版本可联合满足。作者称满足这些公理的层次聚类方法为"可容许方法"（admissible methods），并证明这样的方法存在不可数多个（uncountably many）。文中显式构造了若干可容许方法，包括基于良分离簇（well-separated clusters）的方法以及一种非二元的 single linkage 变体。作者进一步研究可容许方法之间的关系：对某些方法对，一方生成的层次总是精化（refine）另一方生成的层次，这一精化关系定义了可容许方法类上的偏序（partial order）。

Result: 理论研究结果（无数据集实验）：（1）证明层次聚类下三条公理的层次版本可同时满足，与扁平设定下的不可能性形成对比；（2）证明可容许方法存在不可数多个，并显式构造了基于良分离簇的方法及非二元 single linkage 变体；（3）精化关系在可容许方法类上定义了一个偏序；（4）该偏序集不存在最大元素（greatest element），且包含不可数多个两两不可比的极大元素（maximal elements），揭示了可容许方法之间的巨大多样性；（5）这种多样性仍受约束：每个可容许方法都包含充分良分离簇的层次，且任意有限个可容许方法共享一个非平凡公共骨架（common backbone）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将 Kleinberg 不可能性定理从扁平聚类拓展到层次聚类，证明层次设定下公理可联合满足，从而表明不可能性结果是扁平聚类特有的现象而非聚类的普遍规律。作者通过显式构造可容许方法、建立精化偏序、刻画极大元素多样性及公共骨架等结构性质，系统刻画了可容许方法的理论空间。意义在于为层次聚类提供了公理化基础。潜在局限：研究为纯理论性质，未涉及算法计算复杂度、有限样本下的统计性质或实际数据上的经验验证。

Abstract: Despite its ubiquity, clustering lacks a universally accepted definition of what is a cluster. Kleinberg's Impossibility Theorem formalizes this difficulty by showing that no flat clustering method can simultaneously satisfy three natural axioms: scale invariance, richness, and consistency. In this paper, we ask whether this impossibility persists when the output is a hierarchy rather than a single partition. We show that, in contrast to the flat clustering setting, the hierarchical analog of these axioms are jointly satisfiable. In fact, there exist uncountably many hierarchical clustering methods satisfying these axioms, which we call admissible. We explicitly construct several admissible methods, including methods based on well-separated clusters and a non-binary version of single linkage. For certain pairs of admissible methods, the hierarchy produced by one always refines that produced by the other. This refinement relation defines a partial order on the class of admissible methods. This partially ordered set has no greatest element and contains uncountably many pairwise incompatible maximal elements, revealing substantial diversity among admissible methods. Nevertheless, this diversity is constrained: every admissible method contains a hierarchy of sufficiently well-separated clusters, and every finite collection of admissible methods shares such a nontrivial common backbone.

</details>


### [158] [Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES) under Time-Varying Loss Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11207)
*Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CONES（Nested Evolving Feasible Sets）问题的时变损失扩展：在可行域随时间嵌套收缩（S₁⊇S₂⊇…⊇S_T）的同时，允许损失函数 f_t 也随时间变化。作者证明投影近端算法在凸损失下可同时达到 O(T^{1-β}) 遗憾与 O(T^β) 移动代价，在强凸损失下可达到 O(1) 遗憾与 O(log T) 移动代价，并给出相应的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 CONES 框架中目标函数固定、仅可行域嵌套演化，是嵌套凸体追踪（NCBC）的优化推广，目标是在保证始终可行的前提下同时最小化对静态最优的遗憾和总移动代价。现实在线决策（如资源逐渐耗尽的调度、逐步收紧约束的控制问题）中损失函数与约束往往同时变化，因此将 CONES 推广到时变损失更具一般性和实用价值。核心挑战在于遗憾与移动代价之间存在根本性权衡，需要刻画二者的最优折中边界。

Method: 将 CONES 推广为损失函数 f_t 随时间的序列，可行域保持嵌套收缩结构。采用投影近端算法（projected proximal algorithm）作为在线求解器，在每轮进行近端步后投影回当前可行集 S_t。理论分析分两种情形：凸损失下给出遗憾与移动代价的同步上界 O(T^{1-β}) 与 O(T^β)（β∈[0,1)），并证明任何弱自适应算法在 O(T^β) 遗憾下移动代价下界为 Ω(T^{(1-β)/2})；强凸损失下证明可同时取得 O(1) 遗憾与 O(log T) 移动代价，并证明任何具亚线性 anytime 遗憾的在线算法移动代价下界为 Ω(log T)。

Result: 工作为纯理论/最优化分析，无实验数据集或经验评估。主要结果包括：(1) 凸损失下投影近端算法同时实现 O(T^{1-β}) 遗憾与 O(T^β) 移动代价，并给出匹配阶的下界 Ω(T^{(1-β)/2})；(2) 强凸损失下算法达到 O(1) 遗憾与 O(log T) 移动代价，且下界 Ω(log T) 表明该移动代价在阶数上不可进一步改进（对具亚线性 anytime 遗憾的算法）。结果表明随时间演化的损失与嵌套收缩可行域可被统一处理，并刻画了遗憾—移动代价的折中关系。

Conclusion: 本文将 CONES 从固定目标函数推广到时变损失，给出了凸与强凸两种情形下遗憾与移动代价的同时上界及相应下界，深化了对嵌套可行域在线优化中性能权衡的理解，并将 NCBC 的优化视角进一步一般化。局限在于结果以渐近理论界为主，缺乏实验验证，且下界针对弱自适应/anytime 遗憾类算法，实际算法能否在更一般设定（如无强凸性或非凸损失）下达到类似保证仍待探索。

Abstract: Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES)} was introduced in \cite{CONESVaze} where the objective function \(f\) remains fixed but the feasible region evolves over time as a nested sequence \(S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T\). The goal of an online algorithm is to simultaneously minimize the regret with respect to hindsight static optimal benchmark and the total movement cost $M_\cA(T)$ while ensuring feasibility at all times. CONES is an optimization-oriented generalization of the well-known \emph{nested convex body chasing} (NCBC). In this paper, we extend CONES to allow for loss functions $f_t'$s to also change over time. When all loss functions are convex, we show that the projected proximal algorithm achieves $O(T^{1-β}), O(T^β)$ simultaneous regret and movement cost, respectively, for any $β\in [0,1)$, over a time horizon of $T$. We also show that any {\it weakly adaptive} online algorithm with $O(T^β)$ regret has a movement cost of $Ω\left(T^{\frac{1-β}{2}}\right)$ for any $β\in [0,1)$. When all loss functions are strongly convex, we show that the projected proximal algorithm simultaneously achieves $O(1)$ regret and a movement cost of $O(\log T)$. To complement this, we show that any online algorithm with sublinear {\it anytime} regret has a movement cost of $Ω\left(\log T\right)$.

</details>


### [159] [REVA: Reusable Evidence View Aggregation for Context-Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11209)
*Tuan Nguyen,Qiran Hu,Banruo Liu,Khoa D. Doan,Kok-Seng Wong,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REVA（Reusable Evidence View Aggregation）从数据挖掘视角重构RAG检索后压缩：将生成器历史查询-文档-模型交互中的注意力痕迹挖掘为以文档为键、与预算无关的可复用评分库，离线渲染出保留文档顺序的预算特定纯文本视图，在提升生成质量的同时大幅降低压缩延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过检索文档增强LLM的知识密集型应用，但更长的上下文会带来延迟增加、KV缓存显存占用上升和token成本提高。检索后压缩（post-retrieval compression）可缓解这一问题，但现有压缩器存在三方面缺陷：(1) 对每个查询独立操作，无法跨查询复用已有计算；(2) 依赖辅助模型或文本重写；(3) 引入的在线开销可能抵消缩短prompt带来的收益。作者进一步指出现代压缩器相对简单截断的增益并不稳定，且会显著增加推理时延迟，因此需要重新审视该问题的建模范式。

Method: REVA将RAG压缩建模为数据挖掘问题，核心是聚合历史查询-文档-模型交互。具体流程为：(1) 挖掘目标生成器（target generator）的历史注意力痕迹（attention traces），而非依赖外部模型打分；(2) 将token级注意力映射到可读的词单元（word units），便于生成自然文本视图；(3) 对同一文档被重复访问时的词级重要性进行跨次聚合，形成以文档为键的评分库（document-keyed score store）；(4) 该评分库与预算无关（budget-agnostic），可在推理时按任意token预算渲染出预算特定的纯文本视图（budget-specific plain-text views），并保持文档原始顺序与标准RAG接口，无需在线重写。

Result: 在四个代表性基准与多种现代LLM上评估，REVA相比现有先进方法在生成质量上提升1.0–5.8个点；同时将压缩开销降低5.3至15.6倍，额外延迟不足40 ms。论文还通过实验验证了现代压缩器相对简单截断的增益不稳定、且会带来可观的推理延迟。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出以数据挖掘/历史交互聚合为核心的RAG压缩范式，将昂贵的每查询在线压缩转移到可复用的离线评分库，实现质量与效率的兼顾，并保持了标准RAG接口的兼容性与预算灵活性。潜在局限包括：依赖对生成器历史注意力痕迹的采集与持续积累，冷启动或文档访问稀疏时评分库可能不充分；评分与目标生成器绑定，跨模型迁移性需进一步验证；主要面向文本RAG场景，未涉及多模态或事件数据等其它模态。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive large language model (LLM) applications by conditioning generation on retrieved documents, but longer contexts increase latency, key-value (KV) cache memory, and token cost. Post-retrieval compression can reduce this cost, yet existing compressors often operate independently for each query, rely on auxiliary models or rewriting, and introduce online overhead that can offset the benefit of shorter prompts. We revisit RAG compression from a data-mining perspective by aggregating historical query--document--model interactions into reusable evidence views. We first show that modern compressors have unstable gains over simple truncation and can add substantial inference-time latency. We then propose Reusable Evidence View Aggregation (REVA), a framework that mines the target generator's historical attention traces into a document-keyed, budget-agnostic score store. REVA maps token-level attention to readable word units, aggregates importance across repeated document accesses, and renders budget-specific plain-text views that preserve document order and the standard RAG interface. Across four representative benchmarks and modern LLMs, REVA improves generation quality by 1.0--5.8 points over existing advances, while reducing compression overhead by a factor of 5.3 to 15.6, adding less than 40 ms of latency.

</details>


### [160] [Legible Failures: Detecting and Repairing In-Context Binding Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11216)
*Manas Venkata Sai Ravulapalli,Samrath Singh Chadha,Abhinav M. Hari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出：在实体-义务绑定（entity-obligation binding）任务中，即使语言模型给出错误答案，其冻结隐状态中仍线性可解码出正确的提示内绑定信息；这种"知道但没用"的现象普遍存在于16个公开checkpoint中。作者不仅用探针（probe）诊断，还通过探针输出符号构造失败检测分数（AUROC +0.079），并利用探针对残差流进行无标签导向（steering），在全部8个测试模型上平均提升+0.168准确率，表明在in-context绑定场景中探针是可干预、可行动的（actionable），与"探针检测到的错误难以干预"的近期结论形成对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误答案本身无法区分两种失败模式：模型未编码所需信息，还是编码了却在输出时未加以利用（"知道但没说对"）。这一区分对可解释性与可控性都很关键——若信息确实存在于隐状态，则说明失败源于读取/使用环节而非知识缺失，理论上可通过表征干预进行纠正。近期研究（如探针能检测错误但干预无效）暗示探针检测到的错误可能"顽固"，因此需要验证该现象是否在所有设置下成立，尤其是in-context绑定这类提示直接提供答案信息的场景。此外，token存在性与近因性（recency）等表面因素可能被误认为是模型内部正确信息，需要因果性的对照来排除。若能证明此处探针可行动，则可为无标签的推理时纠错与失败检测提供实用机制。

Method: - 任务：实体-义务绑定任务，模型需将提示中给出的实体与相应义务绑定，输出易错。
- 探针流程：在训练fold上拟合线性探针，在验证fold上选择层，在互斥的测试fold上报告结果，避免层选择与评估泄漏。
- 评估规模：16个公开checkpoint，每个用3个随机种子评估。
- 基线：严格present-obligation基线1/K = 0.125（K个候选），用于衡量在模型答错的试次上探针是否仍含正确信息。
- 因果对照：查询-实体反事实（query-entity counterfactual）实验，用以排除token存在性与近因性解释，确认探针读取的是绑定信息而非表面线索。
- 失败检测：用探针输出不一致性的符号构造分数（sign of probe-output disagreement），与模型自身置信度比较，以AUROC为指标。
- 干预：将残差流（residual stream）朝探针解码出的绑定方向进行导向（steering），全程不使用gold label，测试该干预能否提升准确率。
- 关键结论性判据：分别检验"探针置信度"与"探针与模型输出不一致性"两种信号，以区分哪类信号带来增益。

Result: - 在模型答错的试次上，探针准确率比严格present-obligation基线（1/K = 0.125）高出+0.196（95% CI [+0.101, +0.296]，对模型bootstrap），说明隐状态中保留了大量被模型输出忽略的正确绑定信息。
- 查询-实体反事实证明该效应不能由token存在性或近因性解释，支持其为真正的绑定表征。
- 基于探针输出不一致性符号的分数，在失败检测上比模型自身置信度高+0.079 AUROC（95% CI [+0.036, +0.126]）。
- 而原始探针置信度相对模型置信度没有可测量的提升，说明增益来自"探针与模型判断不一致"这一信号，而非探针的绝对置信水平。
- 无标签地对残差流朝探针解码绑定方向进行导向，使全部8个被测试模型的准确率平均提升+0.168（95% CI [+0.066, +0.280]）。

Conclusion: 论文核心贡献有三：一是量化了"模型答错但隐状态含正确答案"这一现象在16个checkpoint上的普遍性与幅度；二是通过反事实实验与失败检测分析，证明该信号有因果意义且可用于改进失败检测（+0.079 AUROC）；三是给出首个（据作者表述）在该设置下探针可行动的正面证据——无gold label的残差流导向带来+0.168平均准确率提升，与近期"探针检测到的错误难以干预"的结论形成重要对照，表明in-context绑定是可干预的有利场景。其意义在于把探针从诊断工具扩展为推理时纠错与失败检测的实用机制。潜在局限：结论限于实体-义务绑定与16个公开checkpoint，未覆盖更大规模或更多任务族；导向的无标签性质依赖探针可靠性，其跨分布泛化能力未被充分刻画；+0.168的效应量在不同模型间的方差与失败模式的多样性仍待进一步分析。此外，该研究针对语言模型表征，与事件相机/SNN/边缘部署等方向无直接关联，属于可解释性与推理时干预范畴。

Abstract: A wrong answer does not show whether the model lacked the needed information or held it and failed to use it. On an entity-obligation binding task, a language model can emit an incorrect prompt-supplied binding while a linear probe can recover the correct one from its frozen hidden state. We measure how often this occurs across 16 public checkpoints, each evaluated with three seeds. We fit a probe on a training fold, select its layer on a validation fold, and report results on a disjoint test fold. On the trials each model gets wrong, probe accuracy exceeds the strict present-obligation baseline, 1/K = 0.125, by +0.196 (95% CI [+0.101, +0.296], bootstrapped over models). A query-entity counterfactual rules out token presence and recency. A score built from the sign of probe-output disagreement improves failure detection over the model's own confidence by +0.079 AUROC (95% CI [+0.036, +0.126]). Raw probe confidence gives no measurable improvement over model confidence. Steering the residual stream toward the probe-decoded binding, with no gold label, raises accuracy on all eight models tested by a mean of +0.168 (95% CI [+0.066, +0.280]). Where recent studies report that probe-detected errors are resistant to interventions, we find that in-context binding is a setting in which probes are actionable.

</details>


### [161] [Polyhedral Geometry of Time-to-First-Spike Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11227)
*Manjot Singh,Guido Montúfar,Gitta Kutyniok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论角度研究脉冲神经网络（SNN）的表示能力，聚焦于 time-to-first-spike（TTFS，首脉冲时间）编码模型。作者证明每个神经元的放电时间可表示为具有指数级、强约束仿射分段的 maxout 型函数，并将因果区域形式化为具有固定因果集的多面体区域，进而给出浅层与多层前馈脉冲网络中因果区域数量的上下界，理论证明脉冲网络能够比传统 ReLU 前馈网络生成更丰富的输入空间划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN 是异步、事件驱动的计算框架，与常规前馈网络互补，且与神经形态硬件、事件相机等异步感知系统天然契合。然而相较于 ReLU 等传统网络的成熟理论（如分段线性区域划分、表达能力界），SNN 的表示能力理论分析仍不充分。作者关注 TTFS 模型下"输入-输出映射连续且分段线性"这一设定，其中各仿射分段的成立由因果可行性约束（哪些突触前脉冲先于神经元放电）决定。理解 SNN 的表达能力，对判断其在复杂时空任务中的潜力及指导架构设计具有重要意义。

Method: 作者采用 TTFS 编码，将神经元放电时间建模为输入的分段线性函数。核心理论工具包括：(1) 证明每个神经元的放电时间具有 maxout 型表示，包含指数级数量、受高度约束的仿射分段；(2) 将"因果区域"形式化为多面体（polyhedral）区域，其定义为由固定因果集合（即放电先后顺序的可行性约束）所确定；(3) 分别在浅层与多层前馈脉冲网络中，推导因果区域最大数量的上界与下界，从而刻画网络对输入空间的划分复杂度。分析围绕因果可行性约束如何决定各仿射分段的生效条件展开。

Result: 本文以理论推导为主，辅以实验验证。主要结论是：脉冲网络能够生成比传统前馈 ReLU 网络更丰富的输入空间划分，即其可实现的线性区域/因果区域数量在相同条件下超过 ReLU 网络的相应界。作者给出了浅层和多层前馈脉冲网络中因果区域数量的上下界，理论与实验均支持这一丰富性结论。摘要未给出具体数据集、评价指标或数值结果。

Conclusion: 论文的贡献在于为 TTFS 脉冲网络建立了表达能力理论：给出神经元放电时间的 maxout 型表示、将因果区域形式化为多面体并给出浅层/多层网络的因果区域数量上下界，证明 SNN 的输入空间划分能力优于 ReLU 前馈网络。其意义在于为 SNN 在学习容量与架构设计上提供了理论支撑，并暗示异步脉冲计算在复杂输入划分任务中可能具备优势。局限在于分析限于 TTFS 编码及连续分段线性映射这一特定设定，结论主要以区域计数的渐近界表述，尚需进一步工作验证这些表达优势在具体任务与硬件上的实际收益。

Abstract: We study the expressivity of spiking neural networks, which provide a natural framework for asynchronous, event-driven computation complementary to conventional feedforward neural networks. We consider the time-to-first-spike model in a setting for which the input-output map is continuous and piecewise linear, with affine pieces governed by causal feasibility constraints that determine which presynaptic spikes occur before a neuron fires. We first show that each neuron's firing time admits a maxout-like representation with exponentially many, highly constrained affine pieces. We then formalize causal regions as polyhedral regions with fixed causal sets and derive upper and lower bounds on the maximal number of causal regions in both shallow and multilayer feedforward spiking networks. Our theoretical and experimental results show that spiking networks can generate richer partitions of the input space than conventional feedforward ReLU networks.

</details>


### [162] [MUtE: A Dual Framework for Concept Erasure and Counterfactual Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11253)
*Antoine Saillenfest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视概念擦除（concept erasure）的最优理论边界，推导出一类能自然诱导确定性双重反事实映射（dual counterfactual mapping）的新擦除函数类，并设计了一种在反事实轨迹上施加平移偏置（translational bias）的实现，从而统一了概念擦除与反事实生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从表示中擦除特定概念信息有助于缓解偏见或解释模型决策。该任务的核心联合目标是：使目标概念在表示中不可预测（unpredictable），同时最大程度保留与概念无关的信息。然而，理论上的最优性与实际表示学习之间存在鸿沟，且概念擦除与反事实生成此前多被割裂处理。论文旨在弥合这一理论与实践的差距，并统一两类任务。

Method: 方法上，作者首先重新推导概念擦除的最优边界，据此得到一类新的擦除函数，该函数类天然诱导出确定性的双重反事实映射。为连接理论最优与实用表示学习，作者设计了一种具体实现：在反事实轨迹上施加平移偏置约束。这一约束的动机在于许多概念在现代语言模型中呈现出几何上的平移结构。该框架使概念擦除与反事实生成之间能够无缝切换与导航。

Result: 论文在实验上验证了该框架的有效性，主要应用于两个场景：提升下游算法公平性（algorithmic fairness）以及生成反事实文本（counterfactual texts）。摘要未给出具体的基线、数据集或量化指标，但明确声称在这两方面均取得改善。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：（1）重新审视并刻画概念擦除的理论最优边界；（2）推导出诱导确定性双重反事实映射的新擦除函数类；（3）提出带平移偏置约束的可实现框架，将概念擦除与反事实生成统一起来，并用于公平性提升与文本反事实生成。潜在局限在于平移偏置的假设依赖概念在表示空间中的几何平移结构，可能不适用于非线性或不满足该几何假设的概念；摘要亦缺乏对实验规模与量化结果的说明。

Abstract: Erasing concept-specific information from representations has been proven useful for mitigating bias or interpreting model decisions. The joint objective is to transform the original representations such that the target concept becomes unpredictable, while maximally preserving concept-unrelated information. In this work, we revisit the optimal bounds of concept erasure to derive a novel class of erasure functions that naturally induce a deterministic, dual counterfactual mapping. Bridging the gap between theoretical optimality and practical representation learning, we design an implementation that imposes a translational bias on counterfactual trajectories - a constraint that aligns with how many concepts geometrically manifest in modern language models. Our framework enables seamless navigation between concept erasure and counterfactual generation. We empirically demonstrate its efficacy in improving downstream algorithmic fairness and generating counterfactual texts.

</details>


### [163] [A Dynamic Fusion Large Language Model for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11314)
*Xue Qiu,Jianli Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态融合大语言模型（DF-LLM）用于交通流预测，通过时空嵌入、图卷积时空融合与带差异化参数适配及上下文聚合注意力的LLM主干，实现多尺度时空特征的协同建模，在四个数据集上取得优于对比方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通流预测是智能交通系统的核心支撑技术，通过历史数据推断未来交通动态，有助于缓解拥堵、提升资源分配效率。然而传统神经网络依赖单一的/模态化特征建模，精度难以突破；而大语言模型（LLM）虽具备强大序列建模能力，却对空间拓扑信息捕捉不足，且对时空相关性的挖掘不充分。因此需要一种能同时融合空间拓扑与动态依赖、并兼顾训练效率与数据适应性的框架。

Method: DF-LLM由三个核心组件构成：(1) 时空嵌入模块，实现多尺度时空特征的协同表示（synergistic representation）；(2) 时空融合模块，通过图卷积（graph convolution）整合空间拓扑结构信息与动态依赖关系；(3) LLM主干，采用差异化参数适配策略（differentiated parameter adaptation）在训练效率与交通数据适应性之间取得平衡。此外，模型引入上下文聚合注意力模块（context aggregation attention）以增强全局依赖建模，并在LLM主干中引入残差连接以缓解深层网络的梯度消失问题。

Result: 论文在四个数据集上进行了实验，以多个评价指标与对比方法进行比较，结果表明DF-LLM在所有四个数据集上的指标均取得更优性能。摘要未明确给出具体数据集名称、评价指标（如MAE/RMSE/MAPE）及数值结果。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将LLM引入交通流预测，并通过时空嵌入、图卷积时空融合、差异化参数适配、上下文聚合注意力及残差连接等设计，弥补LLM在空间拓扑建模与时空相关性挖掘上的不足，同时兼顾训练效率。摘要级别的信息未披露具体数据集、指标数值、计算开销及可复现细节，其通用性与在更大规模/不同城市路网上的泛化能力尚待验证。

Abstract: Traffic flow prediction is a core supporting technology for intelligent transportation systems. It uses historical data to infer future traffic dynamics in specific areas, thereby helping to alleviate congestion and improve resource allocation efficiency. Traditional neural networks struggle to break through accuracy limits due to their reliance on singular feature modeling, while large language models (LLMs) suffer from insufficient capture of spatial topological information and mining spatiotemporal correlation. This study proposes a Dynamic Fusion Large Language Model (DF-LLM) for traffic flow prediction. The model incorporates three core components: spatiotemporal embedding module, spatiotemporal fusion module, and LLM backbone. The spatiotemporal embedding module enables synergistic representation of multi-scale spatiotemporal features. The spatiotemporal fusion module integrates spatial topology and dynamic dependencies via graph convolution. The LLM backbone adopts a differentiated parameter adaptation strategy to balance training efficiency and traffic data adaptability. Additionally, it introduces a context aggregation attention module to strengthens global dependencies. More importantly, the LLM backbone takes the residual connections to mitigate the gradient vanishing in deep networks. Experiments show that DF-LLM has achieved better performance by comparing the metrics on all the four datasets.

</details>


### [164] [Estimating Inconsistency Response Surfaces under Uncertainty in Cyber-Physical System Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11331)
*Johannes Mäkelburg,Tim Schwabe,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将CPS（信息物理系统）在不确定性下的模型不一致性问题重新表述为"干预响应建模"问题，利用Saltelli采样与多保真蒙特卡洛估计生成干预-响应数据集，训练代理模型直接从不确定性的传播几何中预测不一致性，并进一步实现敏感性分析与基于梯度的最小干预一致性恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS通常由多个相互关联的模型表示，开发过程中需保证共享模型元素在不同模型间保持一致。传感器噪声、模型抽象等不确定性会改变模型元素的可行取值，从而引入不一致性（即多个模型无法被联合满足）。现有方法只能针对给定的不确定性配置判断一致性，缺乏在大规模不确定性空间上系统性地探索、分析与解释不一致性来源的能力。因此，如何可扩展地建模不确定性空间中的不一致性分布、定位主导不确定性来源并给出修复方案，成为CPS开发中的关键问题。

Method: 核心思想是将不一致性建模为对不确定性干预的响应函数，而非逐点判断。方法分三步：(1) 使用Saltelli采样在不确定性空间中构造干预配置，并结合多保真蒙特卡洛估计生成"干预-响应"数据集，控制采样与估计成本；(2) 训练代理模型（surrogate model），以传播后的不确定性几何为输入，直接预测不一致性，从而替代昂贵的蒙特卡洛评估；(3) 在学到的响应曲面上进行敏感性分析以识别主导不确定性驱动因素，并提出基于梯度的"一致性恢复（consistency recourse）"方法，求解能恢复一致性的最小不确定性干预。整体构成从采样、估计、代理建模到分析与修复的闭环流程。

Result: 实验覆盖48个场景、10个CPS领域。结果表明：代理模型的不一致性预测与蒙特卡洛估计相匹配，同时将单次评估时间从毫秒级降至微秒级，使得在固定计算预算下可进行数量级更多的响应曲面评估。基于所学习响应曲面的敏感性分析能够识别主导不确定性来源；基于梯度的一致性恢复方法能够确定恢复一致性的最小不确定性干预。整体验证了"不一致性可被学习、分析与修复"这一响应曲面建模范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 将CPS不确定性下的不一致性重新表述为干预响应建模问题；(2) 结合Saltelli采样与多保真蒙特卡洛估计构建代理模型，实现微秒级不一致性预测；(3) 在响应曲面上提供敏感性分析与梯度式一致性恢复，支持定位主导不确定性并给出最小修复干预。其意义在于为CPS开发中的不确定性感知一致性管理提供了可扩展的方法基础。潜在局限包括：方法依赖代理模型的泛化能力与代理精度，代理预测的误差可能影响响应曲面分析与恢复方案的正确性；实验仅覆盖48个场景与10个领域，尚需在更复杂、更高维的不确定性空间和真实工业CPS中进一步验证。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) are commonly represented through multiple interconnected models. During development, CPS consistency requires that shared model elements remain compatible across these models. Uncertainty, for example, due to sensor noise or model abstraction, changes the admissible values of model elements and can introduce inconsistencies, i.e., situations in which models can no longer be jointly satisfied. While existing approaches can determine consistency for a given uncertainty configuration, they provide limited support for systematically exploring, analyzing, and explaining inconsistency across large uncertainty spaces. We address this challenge by reformulating inconsistency as an intervention response modeling problem. Using Saltelli sampling and multi-fidelity Monte Carlo estimation, we generate intervention-response datasets and train a surrogate model that directly predicts inconsistency from the propagated uncertainty geometry. Experiments on 48 scenarios and 10 CPS domains show that the surrogate matches Monte Carlo estimates while reducing evaluation time from milliseconds to microseconds, enabling orders-of-magnitude more response-surface evaluations within fixed computational budgets. Building on the learned response surfaces, we perform sensitivity analysis to identify dominant uncertainty drivers and introduce a gradient-based consistency recourse method to determine minimal uncertainty interventions that restore consistency. The results show that inconsistency under uncertainty can be effectively learned, analyzed, and repaired through response-surface modeling, providing a scalable foundation for uncertainty-aware consistency management in CPS development.

</details>


### [165] [Reification as a Transferable Vocabulary: Zero-Shot Link Prediction with Vanilla GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11347)
*Camille Pradel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将知识图谱基础模型的迁移机制从"架构硬编码"转移到"表示本身"：通过"物化"(reify)输入图——把每条事实变成节点，用固定的六条元关系连接主语、宾语与关系类型节点，从而使标准的教科书级GNN（GAT、GINE、GraphSAGE、R-GCN）无需专门架构即可实现零样本归纳链路预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱基础模型（如ULTRA）依赖专门设计的架构，将跨图迁移机制硬编码在网络结构中。这种做法需要模型参数承载关系类型语义，导致架构复杂、迁移能力受限于模型设计，难以复用现成的通用GNN。作者希望验证：迁移能力是否可以由输入表示而非架构承担，从而让标准GNN也能实现零样本跨图泛化。这一问题对于降低基础模型的架构负担、提升可复用性和可解释性具有意义。

Method: 核心是"物化"(reification)表示：将每条事实(knowledge triples)转换为一个节点，通过一个固定的六条元关系词表（subject、object、relation type等）连接到其主语、宾语和关系类型。关系类型被表示为匿名的共享节点，而非模型参数，因此不同图共享同一套符号词汇，实现零样本迁移。在该表示上，作者使用五种教科书级GNN：GAT、GINE（sum聚合）、GINE（mean+max聚合）、GraphSAGE、R-GCN。每个模型仅在单个含4,245条三元组的知识图上训练30分钟（单张NVIDIA A100）。该固定词表还扩展到关系数据库：每行成为实体，外键列成为关系类型。

Result: 在40个归纳式链路预测基准上做零样本评估。最佳模型为现成的GAT，在ULTRA自己的评估套件上可与专门的基础模型ULTRA（在三个图上预训练）相媲美甚至持平。数据库方面，在三个知识图谱上预训练的同族模型，对两个未见数据库（无单元值、无schema文本、无上下文标签）进行初步探测，其外键目标排序显著高于随机初始化与度数(degree)对照基线。作者开源了全部40个基准的代码、检查点与评估流水线。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于证明跨图零样本迁移机制可以完全由表示承担，而非依赖专门架构——一个简单的物化表示加上现成GAT即可匹配专用基础模型ULTRA。这简化了基础模型的架构假设，并展示了向关系数据库迁移的潜力。潜在局限：单模型仅基于一个含4,245条三元组的小知识图训练，数据库实验为"初步探测"，泛化性、可扩展性及与其他基础模型的全面对比仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Knowledge graph foundation models such as ULTRA achieve zero-shot link prediction on unseen graphs through dedicated architectures that hard-code a transfer mechanism. In this work we move that mechanism out of the architecture and into the representation, by \emph{reifying} the input graph: every fact becomes a node, connected to its subject, object, and relation type through a fixed vocabulary of six meta-relations, with relation types as anonymous shared nodes rather than model parameters. On this representation, five textbook GNNs (GAT, GINE with sum and with mean+max aggregation, GraphSAGE, R-GCN), each trained on a single knowledge graph of 4,245 triples for 30 minutes on one NVIDIA A100, transfer zero-shot to 40 inductive link-prediction benchmarks. The best of them, an off-the-shelf GAT, matches ULTRA, a dedicated foundation model pretrained on three graphs, across ULTRA's own evaluation suite. The same fixed vocabulary extends to relational databases, a row becoming an entity and a foreign-key column a relation type; a preliminary probe on two unseen databases, with no cell values, schema text or in-context labels, shows a model of this family pretrained on three knowledge graphs ranking foreign-key targets far above random-initialization and degree controls. We release the code, the checkpoints, and the evaluation pipeline for all 40 benchmarks.

</details>


### [166] [Local Robustness Quantification for Naive Bayes Classifiers and Generative Forests: a General Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11366)
*Adrián Detavernier,Jasper De Bock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套针对两类概率图模型（PGM）生成式分类器——朴素贝叶斯分类器与生成式森林（随机森林的概率化扩展）——的预测鲁棒性量化方法。鲁棒性被定义为分类器分布在不改变预测结果的前提下可被扰动的最大程度，扰动通过对 PGM 局部模型的参数施加邻域约束（包括 ε-污染、全变差距离球与卡方散度球）来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度与生成式分类器在高风险场景中的部署需要对其预测可信度进行评估。既有鲁棒性研究多集中于判别式模型的输入扰动（如对抗攻击），而对生成式概率模型中"分布层面扰动"如何影响预测稳定性的理论刻画不足。作者认为，一个预测的鲁棒性可以作为其可信度（trustworthiness）的指标，因此需要一个可计算、有理论支撑的鲁棒性量化框架，尤其针对具备可解释概率结构的 PGM 分类器。

Method: 论文遵循"鲁棒性量化"（robustness quantification）范式，将鲁棒性定义为使分类器预测保持不变的分布扰动幅度上界。研究对象为两类生成式分类器：朴素贝叶斯（naive Bayes）与生成式森林（generative forests，即随机森林的概率扩展，用 PGM 表示叶节点上的类条件分布）。扰动建模为对 PGM 中局部模型（local models）的改动，限定在一般邻域内，并重点分析三类具体邻域：ε-污染模型、全变差（total variation）距离球以及卡方（chi-squared）散度球。作者为这些设定推导了可计算的鲁棒性数值计算方法（即求解在给定散度/污染约束下预测翻转的临界扰动半径）。

Result: 论文在多个基准数据集（benchmark datasets）上验证所提方法，计算每个预测对应的鲁棒性数值。实验表明，预测的鲁棒性值与预测正确性之间存在相关性，因而可作为可信度指标；作者将其与其他可信度指标（如置信度、不确定性度量等）进行比较，说明该鲁棒性度量在指示预测可信性方面的有效性（具体数据集名称、数值结果与评价指标未在摘要中给出）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：为朴素贝叶斯与生成式森林两类 PGM 生成式分类器建立了分布层面鲁棒性量化框架，覆盖 ε-污染、总变差与卡方散度多种扰动邻域，并将鲁棒性值用作预测可信度的指示器。其意义在于为生成式概率模型提供了可计算的可靠性度量，补充了以判别式模型为主的对抗鲁棒性研究。局限方面：方法依赖 PGM 的显式概率结构与局部模型可分解假设，可扩展性受限于邻域形式的选取；摘要中未涉及事件相机、时序建模、目标检测/跟踪、小目标、UAV 感知、脉冲神经网络或高效实时系统等方向，与这些主题无明显关联。

Abstract: We provide methods for calculating the robustness of the predictions of two types of generative classifiers whose underlying distribution is a Probabilistic Graphical Model (PGM): naive Bayes classifiers and generative forests (a probabilistic extension of random forests). Following the paradigm of robustness quantification, we define the robustness of a prediction as the extent to which the distribution of the classifier can be perturbed without changing this prediction. We consider perturbations obtained by varying the local models of the PGMs within general neighborhoods and focus in particular on epsilon-contamination, total variation distance and chi-squared divergence balls. We test our methods on benchmark datasets, demonstrate that the robustness value of a prediction serves as an indicator for its trustworthiness and compare our approach with other such indicators.

</details>


### [167] [Prevalence Determines Precision:Silent Contamination in Detector-Defined Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11449)
*Jia Huang,Yankai Wan,Yangjun Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，基于检测器/启发式/模型从候选池中筛选构建的机器学习数据集，其精度受每个候选池中真阳性流行率（prevalence）经贝叶斯公式的支配，而非仅取决于检测器质量；作者用一个检测器在三个候选池上产生的幽灵（phantom/假阳性）事件，实证揭示了污染对数据集精度估计与统计量的系统性扭曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量ML数据集是通过在候选池上运行检测器、启发式规则或模型来构建的：被接受的样本即成为标签。这意味着数据集的"精度"本质上由候选池内真阳性流行率通过贝叶斯关系决定，而检测器质量只是其中一个因素。因此，若忽视不同候选池的流行率差异，就会对数据集精度产生严重误判。论文用同一仪器、同一时期的数据，配备一个独立的官方索引（official index），将每个被检测项标注为真实或幽灵，从而定量刻画这一问题的严重程度，对数据集构建方法论、评测可信度具有基础性意义。

Method: 方法核心是"一个检测器+三个候选池"的控制实验：使用单一检测器与单一时间周期，构建一个检测器定义的事件数据集，并引入一个独立的官方索引对每个检测项进行真/假标注。以此：(1) 分别测量三个池的幽灵率；(2) 用贝叶斯表达式由高幽灵率池向低幽灵率池迁移精度估计，并与实测值对比；(3) 对检测响应曲线做凸组合分解，检验其是否可写成"真实事件分量+幽灵分量"的精确凸组合（考察残差）；(4) 比较不同估计量（estimator）在同一窗口下的行为，分析分母被污染导致的稀释/膨胀效应，以及某种常见归一化把估计量变为"比率之均值"（mean of ratios）后期望可能不存在的问题。

Result: 单一检测器、三个候选池的幽灵率分别为 81.7%、9.0%、0.0%。将两个高幽灵率池的精度迁移到低幽灵率池，预测值为 0.955，而实测仅 0.183，误差高达 +422%；而贝叶斯表达式对三个池的预测误差均在 3.3% 以内。检测响应曲线被证实是真实事件分量与幽灵分量的精确凸组合（残差 1.1e-16），且在该数据中幽灵数量为 473，真实事件为 308，即假阳性多于真事件。污染因此不是加性噪声，而是具有"检测器继承形状"的第二信号，其方向依赖于估计量：在相同窗口上，一个统计量被稀释而另一个被膨胀（因其分母同样被污染）。一种常见归一化使估计量变为比率之均值，其期望可能不存在；在同一批 335 个事件上，该估计量返回 0.40，而良定义的估计量返回 0.10。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 从贝叶斯视角形式化说明了数据集精度由候选池真阳性流行率主导，而非仅由检测器质量决定；(2) 用一个可控实证表明，跨池迁移精度可产生数百百分点的误差，而正确使用贝叶斯关系可将误差压至约 3%;(3) 揭示污染是具检测器继承形状的"第二信号"而非加性噪声，且污染对统计量的影响方向取决于估计量定义；(4) 警示常见归一化会退化为比率之均值，其期望可能不存在，导致报告值失真。意义在于为数据集构建与精度报告提供了可操作的方法论警示。局限方面：结论基于单一检测器、单一仪器与单一时期的数据，幽灵率极端（含81.7%与0.0%）反映特定场景，外推到其他检测器、任务或领域的普遍性需进一步验证；且分析依赖存在一项独立的官方索引作为真值来源。

Abstract: Many ML datasets are constructed by running a detector, heuristic, or model over candidate pools; accepted items become labels. Dataset precision is then governed by true-positive prevalence in each pool via Bayes, not solely by detector quality. Using one instrument and period, we hold a detector-defined event dataset plus an independent official index labeling every detected item as real or phantom. One detector, three pools yield phantom rates 81.7%, 9.0%, and 0.0%. Transferring precision from the two high-rate pools to the low-rate pool predicts 0.955 versus measured 0.183, a +422% error; the Bayes expression predicts all three within 3.3%. The detected response curve is an exact convex combination of a true-event and a phantom component (residual 1.1e-16), with phantoms outnumbering true events 473 to 308, so contamination is a second signal with detector-inherited shape, not additive noise. Contamination direction depends on the estimator: on identical windows one statistic is diluted and another inflated because its denominator is also contaminated. A common normalization turns the estimator into a mean of ratios whose expectation need not exist; on the same 335 events it returns 0.40 where the well-defined estimator returns 0.10.

</details>


### [168] [Combining Synthetic and Real Data for Low-Resource Historical OCR: A Manchu Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11495)
*Yan Hon Michael Chung,Hanlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Manchu, now critically endangered, was one of the principal languages of the Qing empire (1636-1912), and its extensive archival record is increasingly digitized but remains difficult to search and analyze at scale. Previous work showed that vision-language models (VLMs) trained only on synthetic Manchu word images can reach 87.4% word accuracy on real Qing manuscripts and prints, leaving a substantial synthetic-to-real gap. This study examines how synthetic and real historical training data should be combined for low-resource OCR. Using 60,000 synthetic and 20,306 real historical word images, we evaluate three pretrained VLMs and a compact convolutional recurrent neural network (CRNN) under four regimes: synthetic-only, real-only, joint synthetic-real, and sequential synthetic-to-real training, following a common checkpoint-selection and archival evaluation protocol. Introducing real training images raises the leading configurations to between 95.09% and 96.28% word accuracy, while no synthetic-only configuration exceeds 87.92%. Synthetic supplementation substantially improves all three VLMs, whereas its marginal effect for the CRNN is sensitive to the training objective. Joint and sequential training yield broadly similar archival accuracy under the tested practical pipelines. A compact CRNN also reaches the leading performance range once real images are available, showing that model scale alone does not determine recognition accuracy. Finally, complementary errors among strong recognizers allow voting to raise accuracy to 98.27% without additional training, while an eighteenth-century Manchu dictionary provides a principled rule for adjudicating disagreements.

</details>


### [169] [DeFiFlowBench: Benchmarking and Improving Safe Executability in Natural-Language DeFi Workflow Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11504)
*Abhinav Rajeev Kumar,Harshit Arora,Varun Singh,Manikandan Nanjappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DeFiFlowBench，一个包含 207 条团队编写提示词的自然语言 DeFi 工作流合成基准，用于评估 LLM 生成工作流的结构完整性与安全性；作者指出结构有效的 DeFi 工作流仍可能授权高风险交易，并提出 Koan-Safe 框架，通过意图解析、可替换生成器与带默认安全参数的结构化修复来提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言合成 DeFi 工作流（workflow synthesis）虽然在图结构层面可能是有效的（graph coverage、配置完整性），但仍可能产生危险交易。例如基于报价（quote）推导出的滑点边界（slippage bound）无法防止订单本身造成的价格冲击（price impact）。这意味着当前仅基于结构或声明式安全检查的评估方式不足以反映真实执行风险。论文的动机在于：建立执行层面的安全评估基准，并探索让工作流合成系统在实际 EVM 执行中避免不安全交易的方法，从而区分"声明的安全性"（declared safety）与真正的"安全保证"（general guarantee）。

Method: 1) DeFiFlowBench：207 条团队编写提示词的自然语言 DeFi 工作流合成基准，衡量三项指标——图覆盖（graph coverage）、配置完整性（configuration completeness）、声明的安全谓词（declared safety predicates），并在本地 EVM 上实际测试所支持的交易配置。2) 评估三类提示策略：直接提示（direct）、约束提示（constrained）、少样本提示（few-shot）。3) 提出 Koan-Safe：融合仅提示的意图解析器（prompt-only intent parser）、可替换生成器（replaceable generator），以及带默认安全参数的结构化修复（structural repair with default safety parameters），并引入混合（hybrid）变体。4) 通过禁用强制执行（enforcement）的匹配候选消融（matched-candidate ablation），检验安全约束的实际效果；并设计 36 例诊断网格评估策略上限（policy cap）对"宽松既有阈值（permissive existing thresholds）"失效模式的修复能力。

Result: 在固定 5% 价格冲击上限（price-impact cap）条件下，direct、constrained、few-shot 提示在每种配置下分别产生 14–19 次不安全的留出执行（held-out executions）。在 75 条留出工作流提示上，Koan-Safe 混合变体在静态安全代理指标上得分 0.67，最佳基线仅为 0.33；Koan-Safe 在已保存的基准输出上未记录任何不安全执行。匹配候选消融显示，当禁用强制执行时会出现 14–17 次不安全执行，说明安全提升来自实际执行约束而非候选选择。此外，额外测试暴露默认注入的局限：既有的宽松阈值仍可能授权不安全交易；单独评估的策略上限在一个 36 例诊断网格上解决了该失效模式。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：(1) 提出 DeFiFlowBench，将 DeFi 工作流合成评估从结构/声明层面扩展到基于本地 EVM 的执行层面；(2) 提出 Koan-Safe，通过意图解析、可替换生成与结构化修复加默认安全参数，显著降低不安全执行；(3) 通过消融与诊断格验证安全收益来源。意义在于强调显式交易保护（explicit trade protections）与基于执行的评估（execution-based evaluation），并明确区分"声明的安全"与"一般性保证"。局限：默认参数注入在面对既有宽松阈值时可能失效，需依赖策略上限等额外机制；结果主要基于本地 EVM 与已保存基准输出，未涵盖主网或更广泛的真实对抗性场景。

Abstract: A structurally valid DeFi workflow can still authorize a costly trade. We introduce DeFiFlowBench, a benchmark of 207 team-authored prompts for natural-language DeFi workflow synthesis. It measures graph coverage, configuration completeness, and declared safety predicates, then tests supported trade configurations on a local EVM. Direct, constrained, and few-shot prompting produce 14-19 unsafe held-out executions per configuration under a fixed 5% price-impact cap. A slippage bound derived from a quote does not prevent the price impact of the order itself. We propose Koan-Safe, which combines a prompt-only intent parser, a replaceable generator, and structural repair with default safety parameters. On 75 held-out workflow prompts, its hybrid variant scores 0.67 on the static safety proxy, compared with 0.33 for the best baseline. Koan-Safe records no unsafe executions on the saved benchmark outputs. A matched-candidate ablation produces 14-17 unsafe executions when enforcement is disabled. Additional tests expose the limits of default injection: permissive existing thresholds can still authorize unsafe trades. A separately evaluated policy cap addresses this failure on a 36-case diagnostic grid. These results support explicit trade protections and execution-based evaluation, while distinguishing declared safety from a general guarantee.

</details>


### [170] [Generalized Score Matching for Parameter Estimation on Convex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11521)
*Nishanth Shetty,Saisuchith Mahajan,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从最小概率流(Minimum Probability Flow, MPF)学习出发，构造性地推导出定义在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 凸子集上的广义分数匹配(Generalized Score Matching)目标函数，并证明其为二阶的 proper local scoring rule，从而保证最小化该目标即恢复真实密度；同时给出指数族模型下的凸性与有限样本相合性，并在分区函数不可解析求解的约束域上验证了估计与生成建模的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然估计虽在统计上高效，但对未归一化模型需要计算并微分分区函数 $Z(\theta)$，在高维或复杂域上往往不可解析求解。分数匹配通过拟合 score 函数（即对数密度的梯度 $\nabla_x \log p(x)$）巧妙消除了对归一化常数的依赖，因此成为学习未归一化模型的实用替代。然而经典的分数匹配主要针对 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 全空间，在受约束域（如非负数据、凸子集）上如何系统性地推广并保持理论保证尚不清晰。本文正是要厘清广义分数匹配的适用范围，并为约束域上的估计提供理论基础。

Method: 核心思路是从 MPF 学习出发，在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 的凸子集 $C$ 上构造性地推导广义分数匹配目标。关键创新包括：(1) 证明所得目标函数是一个二阶的 proper local scoring rule，即其期望在真实密度处取得最小值，从而在理论上保证最小化时恢复真实密度；(2) 对属于指数族的模型，在标准正则条件下建立目标的凸性以及有限样本估计量的一致性；(3) 展示经典分数匹配与非负数据域的自适应变体（如 Hyvärinen 型修正、非负域上的边界项处理）都能自然地作为该框架的特例出现。该推导的构造性特点揭示了广义分数匹配在各类约束域问题中的统一适用范围。

Result: 论文在分区函数解析不可解的约束域上比较了多种基于广义分数匹配的估计器。实验包括两部分：(1) 对定义在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 凸子集上的指数族模型密度做参数估计；(2) 给出一个生成建模用例以展示框架的更广泛适用性。实验目标是验证所提估计器在无归一化常数可用情形下的估计质量，并支撑凸性与一致性等理论结论。具体数据集、指标与量化结果需参见正文（摘要未给出数值细节）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于以 MPF 为起点统一推导了凸子集上的广义分数匹配，给出 proper local scoring rule（二阶）的理论保证，并针对指数族证明了凸性与有限样本一致性，同时表明经典与领域自适应分数匹配均为其特例，在参数估计和生成建模上均具适用性。意义在于为约束域上的未归一化模型学习提供了原则性框架。受限之处：理论保证主要依赖指数族与标准正则条件，即模型须属于指数族且凸域假设成立；摘要未报告具体数值结果，实际高维或复杂域下的可扩展性与估计效率仍有待正文验证。

Abstract: Maximum likelihood (ML) estimation is a principled and statistically efficient approach for learning probabilistic models. However, for unnormalized models, ML estimation requires evaluating the partition function and differentiating through it, which may not always be tractable. Score matching provides a practically viable alternative that circumvents this obstacle by fitting the score in a way that eliminates dependence on the normalizing constant. We derive the generalized score matching objective on a convex subset of $\mathbb{R}^{d}$ constructively starting from Minimum Probability Flow (MPF) learning, and show how classical score matching as well as domain-adapted variants for non-negative data arise naturally within the proposed framework. We show that the resulting objective is a {\it proper local scoring rule} of second-order, which provides the theoretical guarantee that the true density is recovered when the objective is minimized. Furthermore, for a model belonging to the exponential family, we establish convexity of the objective together with consistency of the finite-sample estimator under standard regularity conditions. Our derivation sheds new light on the scope and applicability of generalized score matching in various problem settings. We compare generalized score matching-based estimators on constrained domains, where the partition function is analytically intractable. We provide experimental results on parameter estimation for model densities belonging to the exponential family defined over convex subsets of $\mathbb{R}^{d}$, and a generative modeling use-case to demonstrate broader applicability of the proposed generalized score matching framework.

</details>


### [171] [Particle GFlowNets: Rethinking Generative Marginalization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11538)
*Tiago da Silva,Diego Mesquita,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.LG
