TL;DR: 本文证明生成边际化模型（MaMs）与生成流网络（GFlowNets）在理论上是等价的，并将MaMs的采样策略扩展到非自回归生成过程，提出"粒子GFlowNets"（Particle GFlowNets），通过基于Gelman-Rubin统计量的全状态rejuvenation准则加速大规模组合空间中的训练收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散分布的高效采样与推理是概率建模的核心难题。MaMs被提出作为任意阶自回归的有效神经采样模型，通过学习持久块Gibbs采样器的边际与条件概率，实现单次前向传播的快速后验评估。此前研究将MaMs视为与GFlowNets截然不同的范式，而GFlowNets是一种成熟的离散随机模型推理框架。本文的动机在于：一方面厘清两者的理论关系，另一方面克服MaMs采样策略局限于自回归过程、且在庞大组合空间中学习收敛缓慢的问题。

Method: 方法上包含两部分核心贡献。第一，作者从理论上证明了MaMs与GFlowNets的等价性，从而统一了这两个此前被认为独立的采样范式。第二，将MaMs的采样策略推广至非自回归生成过程。具体而言，提出了一种自动化的全状态rejuvenation准则：该准则源自Gelman-Rubin收敛诊断统计量，用于判断Gibbs采样器何时应进行完整状态更新（rejuvenation），从而有效加速学习收敛。基于此，作者构建了名为Particle GFlowNets的方法，将粒子式采样与GFlowNets框架结合，通过持久块Gibbs采样与自适应rejuvenation实现高效训练。

Result: 实验表明该方法在大规模组合空间（large combinatorial spaces）中显著加速了训练过程，验证了rejuvenation准则对学习收敛速度的关键作用。摘要未详细列出具体数据集、基线方法与量化指标，但明确强调其相较既有方法在训练效率上的明显提升，尤其体现在高维离散组合优化场景下的收敛加速。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是：（1）建立了MaMs与GFlowNets之间的等价性理论联系，统一了两类离散采样范式；（2）将MaMs采样策略扩展至非自回归过程；（3）提出基于Gelman-Rubin统计量的自动全状态rejuvenation准则；（4）提出Particle GFlowNets并实证其在大组合空间中加速训练的有效性。其意义在于为离散概率建模与神经采样提供了统一视角和更高效的训练机制。潜在局限在于：摘要未给出具体基准对比与规模上限，rejuvenation准则在极大规模空间中的计算开销及其对采样质量的权衡仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Generative Marginalization Models (MaMs) have been recently introduced as efficient neural sampling models for any-order autoregressive modelling of discrete distributions. By learning both the marginal and conditional probabilities of a persistent-block Gibbs sampler, MaMs enable fast posterior evaluation with a single neural network forward pass. While prior work has considered MaMs to be distinct from Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets), a well-established paradigm for inference in discrete stochastic models, we show that they are equivalent. Then, we also extend MaMs' sampling strategy to non-autoregressive generative processes. In particular, we describe an automatic criterion for full-state rejuvenation of the Gibbs sampler, derived from the Gelman-Rubin statistic, which plays a key role in speeding up learning convergence. Our experiments show that our method, called Particle GFlowNets, markedly accelerates training in large combinatorial spaces.

</details>


### [172] [A Dataset and Model for Imputing Water Surface Elevation on a Large and Extremely Sparse Spatiotemporal Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11580)
*Ruben Cartuyvels,Karim Douch,Gabriele Bertoli,Mounia El Baz,Artemis Vrettou,Sébastien Lefèvre,Diego Fernandez Prieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 AmazonSWE——一个用于大规模时空图补全（imputation）的亚马逊河流域水面高程（WSE）数据集，覆盖 19K+ 河段、2016–2026 十年数据，融合多源卫星测高（含 SWOT 宽刈幅传感器），并以地面水文站作为留出验证。作者指出既有时空图补全方法不适配该数据的稀疏性、尺度与有向无环河流拓扑，提出一种简单的双向选择性状态空间模型（SSM），通过采样连通子图、将空间与时间展平为单一 token 序列并加入拓扑感知位置编码，相比已发表的 SWOT 加密方法将 RMSE 降低 18–39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 河流网络水面高程的连续监测对洪水预报、水资源管理和全球水循环研究至关重要，但全球大量区域缺乏地面水文站（in situ gauge），限制了可靠建模框架的建立。卫星测高可缓解该问题，却受制于时间覆盖稀疏。现有工作多为统计与物理建模相结合的 SWOT 加密方法，且已有的时空图补全基准在拓扑结构（有向无环 vs. 一般图）、规模与稀疏度上均与真实河流网络差异巨大，直接迁移效果不佳。因此需要一个真实、大规模、极端稀疏的河流网络基准，并需要针对该拓扑设计的新方法。

Method: 数据侧：AmazonSWE 整合多源经处理的卫星测高观测（含 SWOT 宽刈幅传感器），覆盖亚马逊流域 19K+ 河段、2016–2026 年、逐日观测；由于每天仅不足 1% 河段被观测，稀疏度远高于现有补全基准；河网为有向无环图（DAG）结构，规模也大于已有数据集的图。
方法侧：作者先验证既有时空图补全方法在该拓扑、尺度与稀疏度下失效，随后提出一种简单的双向选择性状态空间模型：对连通子图进行采样，将空间与时间维度展平（flatten）为单一 token 序列，并引入拓扑感知位置编码（topology-aware positional encoding），利用双向选择性 SSM 沿该序列建模长程时空依赖，从而对全部河段给出高程预测。

Result: 在 AmazonSWE 上进行训练与评估，地面水文站数据不参与训练、仅用于留出评估，主要指标为相对地面站的 RMSE。结果表明：现有时空图补全方法在该 DAG 拓扑、超大规模与极端稀疏条件下表现不佳；所提出的双向选择性状态空间模型优于这些方法。与已发表的、将统计与物理建模结合的 SWOT 水面高程加密 SOTA 方法相比，该模型将相对地面站的 RMSE 降低 18–39%，且能够为每一个河段产生预测，而非仅限邻近有充足卫星覆盖的河段。

Conclusion: 主要贡献有两点：（1）发布 AmazonSWE，一个真实、具社会价值的大规模卫星测高时空图补全数据集，同时提供地面站留出评估协议，稀疏度与拓扑结构均对现有基准构成新挑战；（2）提出一种简洁的双向选择性状态空间模型，通过连通子图采样、时空展平 token 序列与拓扑感知位置编码适配有向无环河网，全面超越现有方法并显著降低 RMSE，同时实现全河段稠密预测。局限方面：论文以相对地面站的 RMSE 为主要评价，未在摘要中说明对其他流域、其他高程指标或极端水文事件的泛化能力；方法依赖卫星测高观测的可用性与质量，且双向 SSM 在超长序列上的计算与内存开销有待进一步评估。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring of water surface elevation across river networks is critical for flood forecasting, water resource management, and understanding the global water cycle. Yet, the scarcity of in situ gauges across much of the globe constrains the development of reliable modeling frameworks. Satellite altimetry has the potential to alleviate this problem but its use is currently hindered by sparse temporal coverage. To this end, we introduce AmazonSWE, a dataset for training and evaluating large-scale spatiotemporal graph imputation methods that integrates processed satellite altimetry measurements from a range of sources, including the recent wide-swath SWOT sensor. The dataset covers over 19K river sections and 10 years (2016-2026) in the Amazon river basin, with in situ gauges held out for evaluation. Besides contributing a novel real-world use case with the potential for societal impact, AmazonSWE introduces significant technical challenges: with fewer than 1% of sections observed per day, the dataset is far sparser than existing imputation benchmarks, and its directed acyclic river topology is both structurally different from and larger than graphs in existing datasets. We show that prior spatiotemporal graph imputation methods are not adapted to this topology, scale and sparsity, and propose a simple bidirectional selective state space model that outperforms them by sampling connected subgraphs and flattening space and time into a single token sequence with topology-aware positional encodings. Compared to the state-of-the-art published method for SWOT-based WSE densification, which integrates statistics with physical modeling, our model reduces RMSE against in situ gauges by 18-39%, while producing predictions for every river section rather than only those with sufficient nearby satellite coverage.

</details>


### [173] [LoaDiff: Conditional Generation of Electricity Consumption Time Series for Energy Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11639)
*Mariia Baranova,Adrien Petralia,Etienne Le Naour,Nathan Etourneau,Guillaume Hofmann,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoaDiff 是一个基于扩散模型的生成框架，用于合成全年、亚小时分辨率的住宅智能电表负荷曲线，支持对静态家庭属性与动态时间/温度等上下文变量的灵活条件控制，以在数据受限情境下提供可用的合成用电数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源转型带来分布式发电、电气化家电与需求响应项目的普及，使居民用电行为日益复杂。研究负荷预测、家电识别、需求侧灵活性等任务需要细粒度智能电表数据，但这类数据受严格的访问限制与隐私保护法规约束（如 GDPR）。因此，需要既真实又多样、且不泄露原始用户记录的合成数据替代方案。现有生成方法在长时序（全年）、亚小时分辨率以及多变量条件控制方面的能力尚不充分。

Method: 论文提出 LoaDiff，一种基于扩散（diffusion）的生成模型，用于生成全年、亚小时级（sub-hourly）的智能电表负荷曲线。其核心设计是条件化生成：(1) 静态家庭属性条件，如家电拥有情况（appliance ownership）；(2) 动态上下文条件，包括日历信息（calendar information）与室外温度。通过对这两类变量进行灵活条件控制，模型可生成与家庭特征及环境情境相一致的负荷序列。论文未在摘要中给出具体网络结构细节，但整体为面向长时序连续负荷信号的条件扩散生成框架。

Result: 实验在三个住宅用电数据集上进行，并与多种生成基线方法对比。评估涵盖四个互补维度：(1) 保真度与多样性（fidelity and diversity）；(2) 训练记录记忆化风险（memorization risk），即是否复现训练样本；(3) 下游任务效用，包括负荷预测与家电检测；(4) 在替代温度条件下的条件可控性（conditional controllability）。结果显示 LoaDiff 能生成真实且多样的负荷曲线，在生成质量与有限记忆化证据之间取得良好权衡，保留了对下游能源应用有用的信息，并能随条件变量变化做出连贯一致的响应。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出 LoaDiff 这一面向全年亚小时级智能电表负荷曲线、支持静态与动态多变量条件控制的扩散生成模型，并从保真度/多样性、记忆化风险、下游效用与条件可控性四个维度系统性评估合成数据质量。其意义在于为受隐私法规限制的能源数据共享与下游研究提供可行的合成数据方案。局限方面：摘要未提供具体架构细节、数据集规模与定量指标；亚小时级全年序列的生成计算开销可能较大；且未说明在极端用电行为或不同地域/气候区域的泛化能力。

Abstract: The energy transition is reshaping residential electricity consumption through the increasing adoption of distributed generation, electrified appliances, and demand-response programs. Understanding these evolving behaviors requires access to granular smart-meter data for applications such as load forecasting, appliance detection, and demand-side flexibility analysis. However, such data are subject to strict access restrictions and data-protection regulations. Thus, realistic synthetic alternatives are necessary. In this paper, we introduce LoaDiff, a diffusion-based generative model for year-long, sub-hourly smart-meter load curves. LoaDiff supports flexible conditioning on static household attributes, such as appliance ownership, and dynamic contextual variables, including calendar information and outdoor temperature. We evaluate the model against multiple generative baselines on three residential electricity-consumption datasets. Our experiments assess four complementary dimensions: fidelity and diversity, training-record memorization risk, downstream utility for load forecasting and appliance detection, and conditional controllability under alternative temperature conditions. The results show that LoaDiff generates realistic and diverse load profiles, achieves a favorable trade-off between generation quality and limited evidence of memorization, preserves information useful for downstream energy applications, and responds coherently to changes in conditioning variables.

</details>


### [174] [RDDMPI: Residual Denoising Diffusion Model for Probabilistic Multivariate Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11648)
*Ramiro Valdes Jara,David Chapman,Adam Meyers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RDDMPI 提出一种条件残差扩散框架用于多元时间序列插补（MTSI）：不再在原始数据空间直接扩散，而是将插补问题重构为"基线-残差"分解，由预训练确定性插补器捕获主导信号，扩散过程仅建模残差不确定性，并辅以可靠性感知的条件机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列插补需要恢复多个相互依赖变量中的缺失值，在医疗监测、交通网络、能源系统等场景中至关重要。现有扩散式方法直接在原始数据空间进行迭代去噪，迫使去噪网络同时学习全局结构、时间动态与随机变异性，生成任务过于复杂。而现代确定性插补器已能提供较准确的初始重建，因此直接在完整信号空间建模的概率扩散带来了不必要的学习负担。

Method: 核心思路是将概率插补重述为基线-残差分解：由一个预训练模型给出基线（主导信号）重建，扩散过程只负责对残差不确定性建模，从而简化扩散学习目标，使其聚焦于结构化修正项而非重建整个信号。在反向去噪过程中，模型同时以基线补全信号及其隐表示作为条件（确定性引导），并引入可靠性感知的条件机制，自适应地调节基线信息在残差生成中的影响权重，避免不可靠基线误导生成过程。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上进行实验，评估涵盖重建精度与不确定性量化两方面。结果表明 RDDMPI 相较已有方法在两项指标上均取得一致提升，说明残差空间扩散配合可靠性感知条件可以有效改善概率插补性能。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献在于提出残差空间的条件扩散范式，将确定性插补器的强先验与扩散模型的概率建模能力解耦结合，并设计可靠性感知条件以自适应利用基线信息。其意义在于降低扩散生成任务的复杂度、统一精度与不确定性建模；摘要未给出具体数据集、骨干网络与量化数值，其在不同缺失模式与数据模态下的泛化性仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Multivariate time series imputation (MTSI) aims to recover missing values in temporal data composed of multiple interdependent variables. This problem is central to real-world applications such as healthcare monitoring, traffic networks, and energy systems. Recent diffusion-based approaches have shown strong potential for probabilistic imputation by learning to generate missing values through iterative denoising. However, most existing approaches perform diffusion directly in the original data space, requiring the denoising network to simultaneously capture global structure, temporal dynamics, and stochastic variability. This makes the generative task unnecessarily complex, especially when modern deterministic imputers can already provide accurate initial reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose RDDMPI, a conditional residual diffusion framework that operates directly in residual space. Instead of modeling the full missing signal directly, we reformulate probabilistic imputation as a baseline-residual decomposition, where a pretrained model captures the dominant signal and a diffusion process models the residual uncertainty. To better exploit deterministic guidance, \model{} conditions the reverse denoising process on both the baseline-completed signal and its latent representation, while a reliability-aware conditioning mechanism adaptively controls the influence of baseline information during residual generation. This formulation simplifies the diffusion learning objective, enabling it to focus on structured correction terms rather than reconstructing the full signal. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that RDDMPI consistently improves both reconstruction accuracy and uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [175] [Musec: MomentUm SpEctral Clipping for Stable Muon-type Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11655)
*Zhuanghua Liu,Menglian Wang,Luo Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Musec（MomentUm SpEctral Clipping），用“谱裁剪”替代 Muon 优化器中的“谱展平”，并给出高效的优化器层面、架构无关的稳定性机制；同时提出 Soft Musec 及首个面向非凸非光滑优化的 Muon 类方法收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 在大语言模型训练中相较 Adam/AdamW 常取得更优的收敛与性能，但其“谱展平”（将动量矩阵所有奇异值压至约 1）会导致训练不稳定，表现为 loss 尖峰与模型权重无界增长。现有方法多依赖权重裁剪或 attention-logit 裁剪，需要针对特定架构做修改，且无法覆盖所有模型组件，因此缺乏通用、优化器层面的稳定化手段。

Method: Musec 的核心思想是在优化器层面把 Muon 的谱展平改为谱裁剪：不再将动量矩阵的所有奇异值统一置为约 1，而是仅对超过阈值的奇异值进行裁剪，从而保留动量本身的谱结构。作者进一步提出 Soft Musec，用光滑的谱饱和函数替代硬阈值裁剪，并通过耦合 Newton–Schulz 迭代对该函数进行高效近似，避免昂贵的 SVD 计算。该方法与架构解耦，可直接替换 Muon 的更新步骤。理论上，作者在非凸非光滑随机优化框架下建立了 Musec 的收敛性保证，据称是 Muon 类方法在该设定下的首个收敛性证明。

Result: 实验在不同学习率范围与多种模型规模下对比了现有 Muon 变体（如原始 Muon 及基于裁剪的变体）。结果表明 Soft Musec 在稳定性上持续优于已有 Muon 变体：在现有变体出现发散或 loss 尖峰的设置下 Soft Musec 仍保持稳定，而在调参充分的配置下其性能与现有变体相当（匹配其表现）。评估以训练稳定性（loss 曲线、权重增长）与最终性能为主要指标。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献包括：(1) 提出谱裁剪这一优化器层面的架构无关稳定机制，保留动量谱结构而非强制展平；(2) 给出基于耦合 Newton–Schulz 的高效实现 Soft Musec；(3) 建立非凸非光滑随机优化下 Muon 类方法的首个收敛保证；(4) 在多种学习率与模型规模下验证稳定性提升。可能的局限在于：验证集中于语言模型训练，尚未验证在视觉、事件相机或机器人等非 LLM 任务上的泛化性；阈值等超参的敏感性、以及与注意力/权重裁剪方法结合的互补性仍有待进一步分析。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a highly effective optimizer for large language model training, often achieving superior convergence and performance compared with the widely adopted Adam and AdamW optimizers. Nevertheless, Muon is prone to training instability due to its spectral flattening, manifested by loss spikes and unbounded growth of model weights. Existing approaches primarily rely on weight or attention-logit clipping, which require architecture-specific modifications and do not directly address instability across all model components. We propose MomentUm SpEctral Clipping (Musec), which replaces Muon's spectral flattening with spectral clipping: rather than setting all singular values of the momentum matrix to approximately one, Musec clips singular values that exceed a threshold while preserving the underlying spectral structure of the momentum. Our strategy provides an optimizer-level, architecture-agnostic mechanism for stabilizing Muon training. We further develop Soft Musec, an efficient implementation that uses a smooth spectral saturation function approximated by coupled Newton-Schulz iterations. Theoretically, we establish convergence guarantees for Musec in nonconvex nonsmooth stochastic optimization. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first convergence guarantee for Muon-type methods in the nonconvex nonsmooth setting. We provide empirical studies to show that Soft Musec consistently improves training stability over existing Muon variants across a wide range of learning rates and model sizes. Notably, Soft Musec remains stable in settings where existing Muon variants diverge, while matching their performance under well-tuned configurations.

</details>


### [176] [Learnware and AI Model Management System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11656)
*Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从"AI模型存储"走向"AI模型管理"的范式转变，以"学件（Learnware = 模型 + 规约）"替代单一模型作为管理的基本单元，并构建"学件坞系统（Learnware Dock System, LDS）"作为实现路径，使不同开发者、任务、数据与目标下训练的模型可被识别、复用乃至组装协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文类比文件存储到数据库管理系统的演进：数据库将"存储数据"转变为"受管理的资源"，而当前模型池（model pools）本质上仍是"AI模型存储系统"，缺乏管理能力。理想的管理系统需满足两个约束：不访问模型开发者的训练数据，最好也不访问未来用户的原始数据。但核心难题在于——即便是训练模型的开发者本人，也未必完全理解现代AI模型的功能边界，因此在无数据访问的前提下，系统难以判断某模型对给定用户任务是否有用，更难以组装为不同目的独立开发的模型。此问题对模型复用、组合与智能体协作具有重要意义。

Method: 核心思路是将管理的基本单位从"机器学习模型"升级为"学件（learnware）"，定义为 Learnware = Model + Specification（模型 + 规约）。规约（specification）通过一个机器学习过程生成，在生成过程中不泄露开发者的训练数据，并具备理论上的数据保护（data-preservation）性质。由于规约依据公开发布的参照（published reference）生成，因而在不同模型之间具有可比性。这种可比性使规约可进一步充当AI模型协作协议（collaboration protocol），支撑独立开发的模型乃至智能体之间的协同。学件坞系统（LDS）即为实现上述AI模型管理系统的具体路径。

Result: 该文为立场/框架性论文（position/framework paper），主要阐述范式与体系设计，而非以基准实验数据为核心。其关键论点包括：规约的生成具备理论上的数据保持性质；规约因按统一公开参照生成而具备跨模型可比性；这些性质共同支撑模型识别、复用、组装与协作。文中未给出具体的实验数据集、评价指标与量化结果。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于提出"AI模型管理"的问题设定，并给出以"学件"为基本单元、以"规约"为可比与协作中介、以"学件坞系统"为载体的解决路径，试图在保护开发者训练数据与用户原始数据的前提下实现模型的识别、复用与组装。其意义在于为模型复用、模型组合与多智能体协作提供统一协议视角。局限在于：文中所述数据保持性质与可比性偏重理论论证，缺乏具体实验评估；规约的表达能力能否覆盖现代深度模型（如大规模视觉模型、事件相机感知模型）的功能多样性，以及组装协作在真实任务中的有效性，仍需进一步的实证验证。

Abstract: The transition from file storage to database management systems transformed stored data into managed resources. AI now faces an analogous transition from AI model storage to AI model management. Existing model pools essentially serve as \textit{AI model storage systems}. What is needed instead are \textit{AI model management systems} that enable models trained by different developers, for different tasks, with different data, and under different objectives to be identified, reused, and even assembled to address future user tasks. Because AI model developers are generally unwilling to share their training data, such systems should operate without accessing the training data of model developers and, ideally, without accessing raw data of future users. This requirement poses a fundamental challenge: the functionality of a modern AI model may not be fully understood even by the developer who trained it. How, then, can a system identify which models are useful for a given user task, let alone assemble models developed independently for different purposes? At first glance, this objective may appear unattainable. It becomes possible, however, by upgrading the basic unit of management from a machine learning model to a \textit{learnware}. \textit{Learnware = Model + Specification}. The specification, whose assignment transforms a trained model into a learnware, is generated with the help of a machine learning process without disclosing the training data of the developer and has a theoretically established data-preservation property. The \textit{Learnware Dock System (LDS)} provides a path toward powerful AI model management systems. Because specifications are generated according to a published reference and are comparable across models, they can also serve as an AI model \textit{collaboration protocol} through which independently developed models, including intelligent agents, can collaborate.

</details>


### [177] [Why Does Post-Training Quantization Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11716)
*Yuxiang Chen,Michael Beyer,Jun Zhu,Jianfei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了"训练后量化（PTQ）为何有效"这一基础问题。作者通过对比全精度与量化前向传播，识别出两个机制：一是单层新引入的误差倾向于抵消从输入继承的误差（counteracting residual interaction），从而减缓隐藏状态误差随深度的累积；二是 LM-head 的几何结构优先保留高排名 token 的分数与概率，使得模型最自信的预测受影响最小。二者共同解释了为何经过多层传播的量化误差最终只带来很小的输出变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）的训练后量化通过降低权重精度来压缩模型，但每个量化权重都会向隐藏状态中注入误差。直观上，这些误差应随网络深度累积并破坏下一 token 预测；然而实验观察发现，随机初始化的模型会迅速累积误差，而量化后的预训练模型却只累积很小的隐藏状态误差，且下游任务性能基本保持不变——尽管这些模型从未在量化噪声下训练过。这一"反直觉的鲁棒性"缺乏机理解释，论文旨在回答：为什么训练后量化在预训练模型上能够奏效。

Method: 论文采用全精度与量化前向传播的逐层对比分析（discrepancy analysis）作为核心方法。其提出的两个关键机制为：（1）反作用残差交互（counteracting residual interaction）：某一层新引入的误差倾向于与其从该层输入继承的误差方向相反，二者部分相互抵消，使得全精度与量化前向传播之间的差异增长缓慢；该性质在预训练过程中逐渐形成，而非随机初始化模型所具备，定量分析表明它是减缓隐藏误差增长的主要因素。（2）LM-head 几何结构：输出投影层的几何特性优先保留高排名 token 的得分与概率，这些通常对应模型最自信的预测，因此输出端的敏感度对量化噪声具有选择性。研究进一步在不同模型与不同量化设置下验证这些发现的普遍性。

Result: 论文在多种模型与多种量化配置下进行了验证。核心发现是：隐藏状态中的量化误差随层数增长缓慢而非迅速累积，这一现象与"新引入误差抵消继承误差"的层内交互机制相关，且该机制是预训练带来的性质（随机初始化模型中不存在）。同时，LM-head 对高排名 token 分数与概率的优先保留，使得输出层的有效变化进一步被抑制。两项机制共同解释了为何穿越众多层的量化误差仍只产生很小的输出扰动，并保持下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于从机理层面解释了预训练 LLM 对训练后量化的鲁棒性，提出并量化了"反作用残差交互"与"LM-head 几何选择性"两个机制，为理解量化误差在网络中的传播提供了新的分析框架。其意义在于将量化鲁棒性归因于预训练所塑造的层间误差抵消结构与输出端的置信度选择性，而非单纯的数值容错。潜在局限（基于摘要的合理推断）：分析聚焦于前向误差传播与输出端保留性，摘要未涉及激活量化、注意力机制内部误差或极低比特（如 2-bit 以下）场景下的适用边界。

Abstract: Post-training quantization compresses large language models (LLMs) by storing their weights at reduced precision, and each quantized weight introduces an error into the hidden states. Naively, these errors should accumulate with depth and corrupt next-token prediction; randomly initialized models accumulate these discrepancies rapidly, whereas quantized pretrained models accumulate much less hidden-state error and largely maintain downstream task performance, even though they were never trained with quantization noise. This raises the question we address: why does post-training quantization work? Comparing full-precision and quantized forward passes, we identify two mechanisms that characterize pretrained quantization robustness. First, the error a layer newly introduces tends to oppose the error it inherits from the layer's input. The two cancel partially such that the discrepancy between full-precision and quantized passes grows slowly. This counteracting residual interaction develops during pretraining. Our quantitative analysis identifies it as a major factor slowing hidden-error growth. Second, LM-head geometry preferentially preserves the scores and probabilities of high-ranked tokens, which typically represent the model's most confident predictions. Together, these mechanisms explain why quantization error that passes through numerous layers can still produce only small output changes, and we verify the findings across models and quantization settings.

</details>


### [178] [Dynamic language model representations for multi-objective reaction optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11790)
*Joshua W. Sin,David Ming Segura,Bojana Ranković,Siu Lun Chau,Marius D. R. Lutz,Andrea Anelli,Ryan P. Burwood,Kurt Püntener,Maximilian J. Notheis,Raphael Bigler,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种从文本中动态学习化学反应表征的方法：用微调的语言模型对反应条件的文本描述进行编码，并与高斯过程代理模型联合训练，在贝叶斯优化循环中形成任务自适应的表征，从而绕开手工设计shared reaction representation的难题，并在多目标反应优化中减少所需实验次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学合成优化通常需同时兼顾产率、选择性与安全性等多个目标。现有模型驱动方法严重依赖反应组分的表征方式：one-hot编码缺乏化学信息，而分子描述符难以跨化学性质不同的组分推广。当反应涉及结构功能多样（如不同配体齿合度、异相添加剂、不同金属催化剂家族）的组分时，何为合适的共享表征并不明确；而且构建这种表征本身是一个研究难题，且每换一个反应体系都要重新设计。作者希望绕过这一表征设计步骤，直接从文本数据中学习反应表征。

Method: 核心思路是不手工构造反应表征，而是从文本中学习。将反应条件的文本描述输入一个微调的语言模型进行编码，该语言模型与高斯过程(Gaussian Process, GP)代理模型联合训练。编码器产生的表征随之在贝叶斯优化(Bayesian optimization)回路中被端到端地适配到具体任务，形成task-adaptive representation，支持多目标优化。这样表征随优化任务动态调整，无需为每个反应体系重新设计描述符。

Result: 在镍催化和钯催化的交叉偶联反应上评估，覆盖顺序实验和并行实验两种模式。该方法比描述符库和one-hot编码用更少的实验达到优化收敛。前瞻性验证包括：钯催化氰化反应（涉及混合配体齿合度和异相添加剂）以及三目标不对称氢化（跨越手性铱和钌催化剂家族）。两轮高通量实验(192个反应，低于各自设计空间的3%)所得条件可直接放大到克级规模，分别获得94%和84%的分离产率，后者对映体过量(e.e.)达99.6%。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于用文本驱动的联合训练替代手工反应表征，实现跨化学多样组分的任务自适应表征，并在多目标贝叶斯优化中显著减少实验次数，前瞻验证结果可直接放大。意义在于为反应优化提供了一条绕过表征设计瓶颈的通用途径。潜在局限：方法依赖反应条件的文本描述质量与覆盖度，语言模型和GP的联合训练及微调可能带来额外计算成本，且验证集中在交叉偶联、氰化与不对称氢化等特定反应类型，跨更广反应空间的普适性仍待检验。

Abstract: Optimising chemical reactions across multiple objectives, such as yield, selectivity, and safety, is central to chemical synthesis, and model-driven approaches depend critically on how reaction components are represented. Established featurisations are either chemically uninformative, as with one-hot encodings, or, as with molecular descriptors, do not readily extend across chemically distinct components. For structurally and functionally diverse components, it is therefore unclear what a shared representation should contain. Constructing such a representation is itself a challenging research undertaking that must be revisited for each new reaction system. Here we bypass this step by learning the reaction representation dynamically from text. Textual descriptions of reaction conditions are encoded by a fine-tuned language model trained jointly with Gaussian process surrogates, yielding task-adaptive representations within a multi-objective Bayesian optimisation loop. Across nickel- and palladium-catalysed cross-couplings in both sequential and parallel experimentation regimes, this approach reaches optimisation convergence in fewer experiments than descriptor libraries or one-hot encoding. Applied prospectively to a palladium-catalysed cyanation spanning mixed ligand denticity and heterogeneous additives, and to a three-objective asymmetric hydrogenation across chiral iridium and ruthenium catalyst families, two rounds of high-throughput experimentation (192 reactions, under 3% of each design space) delivered conditions translating directly to gram scale in 94% and 84% isolated yield, the latter at 99.6% enantiomeric excess.

</details>


### [179] [Thinking with Looped Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11801)
*Ayhan Suleymanzade,Chanhyuk Lee,Floor Eijkelboom,Nicholas M. Boffi,İsmail İlkan Ceylan,Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Looped Flows，用局部去噪目标训练循环模型的递归更新，从而绕开长程 BPTT 训练难题；推理时将其表述为对由学习去噪器参数化的概率流速度进行积分，通过更细的时间网格花费更多计算来求解更难问题，并支持从不同初始噪声得到多个有效预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类和机器常通过"花更多时间计算"来攻克更难问题。深度学习中，循环/looped 模型在推理时反复更新隐状态正是这一思想的体现。但实践中训练反向传播只穿透一步或几步更新，导致早期更新难以被训练去支持后续更新——梯度无法覆盖足够多步递归，使模型学不到真正能随时间传递有用计算的循环状态。这一训练-推理不匹配限制了循环模型在难推理任务上的能力。

Method: 核心是"looped flows"：不依赖长程时间反向传播，而用局部去噪目标训练递归。关键设计是跨去噪目标建立时序关联——通过渐进递减的噪声水平以及共享噪声，让各步去噪任务彼此关联，从而激励模型学到能够随时间迁移有用计算的循环状态，即使梯度只覆盖少数几步更新也有效。推理阶段被表述为对一条由学到的去噪器参数化的概率流（probability flow）的速度进行积分，并耦合循环状态；推理计算量由时间网格精细度控制，更细的网格即更多计算，从而应对更难的问题；同时由于存在初始噪声采样，可从不同噪声起点产生多个有效预测（对应多解任务）。

Result: 在六个推理基准（含两个多解基准）上评测。Looped Flows 整体上超越此前最先进的 looped 模型，在 ARC-AGI-1 上取得 58.8% 测试准确率，在 ARC-AGI-2 上取得 12.2%。多解基准的结果与其支持从不同初始噪声生成多个有效预测的能力相一致。

Conclusion: 贡献在于：以局部去噪目标替代长程 BPTT 来训练循环递归，缓解了早期更新无法支持后续更新的问题；并将推理形式化为概率流速度积分，使计算量可随问题难度伸缩，且天然支持多解采样。其意义在于为 looped/循环式推理模型提供了一条可扩展的训练与推理范式，在 ARC 类抽象推理基准上取得新的最优结果。局限方面：摘要未报告训练成本、收敛性或对超参数（噪声调度、时间网格）的敏感性，也未说明方法在 ARC 之外任务的泛化程度。

Abstract: Humans and machines often solve harder problems by spending more time on computation. In deep learning, looped models implement this idea during inference by recurrently updating a hidden state. In practice, however, their training backpropagates through only one or a few updates, making it hard to train early updates to support future ones. We propose looped flows, an approach that sidesteps this issue by training the recurrence with local denoising objectives. By imposing temporal association across denoising objectives through progressively decreasing noise levels and shared noise, the model is incentivized to learn recurrent states that transfer useful computation over time, even when gradients cover only a few updates. We then formulate inference as integrating the velocity of a probability flow parameterized by the learned denoiser, coupled with recurrent states. This allows solving harder problems by spending more computation through a finer temporal grid and enables multiple valid predictions from different initial noise samples. Across six reasoning benchmarks including two multi-solution benchmarks, looped flows outperform prior state-of-the-art looped models overall, achieving 58.8% test accuracy on ARC-AGI-1 and 12.2% on ARC-AGI-2.

</details>


### [180] [Model-Aware Schedules Improve Generation via Fiberwise Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11842)
*Luyi Jia,Boyan Zhang,Yilun Liu,Steffen Rulands*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出基于 fiberwise 最优传输的"模型感知"扩散/流匹配调度构造方法：在概率路径的每个时刻与状态处，把兼容的信号/噪声分解视为仿射纤维（affine fiber），在纤维内以最优传输代价衡量预测器诱导分解与真实分解之间的 fiberwise 预测风险；将该风险与系数路径动能作用（kinetic action）结合，可得到闭式的最优时间分配（schedule）。该构造可推广到一般线性预测目标，风险剖面可由早期基线 checkpoint 估计，并在 CIFAR-10 流匹配上以 16 次函数评估实现 38.6% 的 FID 相对下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型与 flow matching 的调度（schedule）决定信号与噪声系数如何沿仿射概率路径混合数据与噪声，直接影响生成质量与采样效率。已有工作通过最小化定义在系数路径上的 kinetic action（受最优传输启发）来解释强基线调度，但这类方法本质上是 model-agnostic 的：它只优化系数路径本身，忽略了预测器（网络）实际的预测误差，因此无法针对具体模型给出最优调度。论文的动机就是构建一种 model-aware 的调度设计，使时间分配同时考虑系数路径的传输代价与模型在给定纤维方向上的预测风险。

Method: 核心构造围绕 fiberwise optimal transport 展开：(1) 在概率路径上的固定时间与状态处，所有与当前边缘分布兼容的信号/噪声分解构成一个仿射纤维（affine fiber），即同一状态可由不同信号-噪声比例实现。(2) 在该纤维内，对真实分解与预测器诱导分解之间的最优传输代价取平均，定义 fiberwise 预测风险（prediction risk）。(3) 将该风险与系数路径的 kinetic action 组合在固定系数曲线上，导出闭式的最优时间分配解（closed-form optimal time allocation）。(4) 该构造可推广至一般线性预测目标（如不同 x0/ε/velocity 参数化）。(5) 风险剖面（risk profile）可从早期训练的基线 checkpoint 中估计，无需额外训练。(6) 论文进一步观察到，各 model-agnostic kinetic 基线会确定各自的 kinetic 参考坐标；在这些坐标下，独立训练的不同模型/设置所得的 fiberwise 风险剖面经单位面积归一化后高度一致，且训练中使用的调度形变也一致，暗示存在经验上的普适性。(7) 据此提出一个冻结的解析分配模板（frozen analytic allocation template），无需再做风险估计或模型特定拟合即可保留大部分 model-aware 增益。

Result: 实验在 DDPM 与 flow matching 上评估，覆盖不同预测目标（prediction targets）、训练配置、风险估计所需的 checkpoint 阶段、数据集与网络架构。主要结果：在 CIFAR-10 的 flow matching 上，16 次函数评估（NFE）时 FID 相对降低 38.6%；model-aware 调度一致优于强基线（含基于 kinetic action 的 model-agnostic 调度）。分析还显示，在各自 kinetic 参考坐标下，来自不同设置、独立训练的模型的归一化 fiberwise 风险剖面高度对齐；相应的调度形变也彼此对齐。该归一化风险一致性在更大规模的 conditional latent diffusion 模型与 2-RF 模型上，通过预训练 checkpoint 诊断得到进一步验证。冻结的解析分配模板可在不额外估计风险、不做模型特定拟合的前提下保留大部分改进。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出一种 model-aware 的调度构造范式：以 fiberwise 最优传输定义预测风险，并将其与系数路径 kinetic action 结合得到闭式最优时间分配，从而在扩散与流匹配中同时考虑传输几何与模型预测误差；实证上在多种参数化、数据集与架构上稳定超越 model-agnostic kinetic 基线，并在低 NFE 场景（16 次评估）取得显著 FID 改进。其意义在于揭示不同模型/设置下风险剖面与调度形变在归一化后具有经验普适性，并提供可复用的解析模板，降低实用门槛。潜在局限：风险剖面需依赖早期基线 checkpoint 估计，依赖模型与训练轨迹；普适性结论主要基于文中所评估的模型与设置（含 conditional latent diffusion、2-RF），更广泛架构与任务上的适用性仍需验证；改进幅度以 FID 等生成指标为主，对其他下游指标与训练成本的影响未充分展开。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching schedules control the signal and noise coefficients that mix data and noise along affine probability paths. Minimizing a kinetic action defined on coefficient paths, motivated by optimal transport, helps explain strong baselines but remains model-agnostic and ignores prediction error. Here we introduce a model-aware schedule construction based on fiberwise optimal transport. At a fixed time and state on the probability path, compatible signal/noise decompositions form an affine fiber. We define a fiberwise prediction risk by averaging optimal-transport costs between the true and predictor-induced decompositions within these fibers. On a fixed coefficient curve, combining this risk with coefficient-path kinetic action yields a closed-form optimal time allocation. This construction extends to general linear prediction targets, and the risk profile can be estimated from an early baseline checkpoint. We evaluate DDPMs and flow matching across prediction targets, training configurations, risk-estimation checkpoints, datasets, and architectures. Our model-aware schedules consistently outperform strong baselines, including a 38.6% relative FID reduction for flow matching on CIFAR-10 at 16 function evaluations. Each model-agnostic kinetic baseline determines its own kinetic reference coordinate. In these coordinates, fiberwise-risk profiles from independently trained models in different settings align closely after normalization to unit area. The resulting schedule deformations used in training also align, suggesting empirical universality across the evaluated models and settings. Pretrained-checkpoint diagnostics extend this normalized-risk agreement to larger conditional latent diffusion and 2-RF models. A frozen analytic allocation template retains most of the model-aware improvement without further risk estimation or model-specific fitting.

</details>


### [181] [AdamX: Cosine similarity meets gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11867)
*Francisco Caldas,Ruben Belo,Cláudia Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 AdamX，一种将余弦相似度作为自适应机制来控制更新幅度的一阶优化器，并引入方差校正方案以在训练早期实现更平滑的优化，方法具有可扩展性和模型无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习训练中，优化器的收敛效率与稳定性至关重要。现有自适应优化器（如 Adam 类）在控制更新幅度、平衡早期训练稳定性方面仍有改进空间。作者希望设计一种通用、易于集成到现有训练流程的优化器，从而在固定超参数预算下加快收敛并提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 AdamX 优化器，核心创新包括两部分：(1) 利用余弦相似度作为自适应机制，动态控制参数更新的幅度；(2) 引入方差校正（variance rectification）方案，在训练初期促进更平滑的优化轨迹，抑制早期不稳定。该方法是模型无关的，可直接替换现有训练流程中的优化器，无需修改网络结构，且具备良好的可扩展性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和网络架构上进行了评估。评价指标为在固定超参数预算下，达到预设性能阈值所需的训练轮数（epochs）。实验表明，AdamX 在收敛速度方面具有竞争力；作者同时公开了代码与实验实现（github.com/FranciscoCaldas/adamX）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出了一种结合余弦相似度自适应与方差校正的通用一阶优化器 AdamX，验证了其在多个基准上的竞争性收敛表现，且易于集成。局限在于摘要未具体说明所测试的数据集、架构、基线对比对象及阈值设定，也缺乏理论收敛性证明，需参考正文验证其普适性与相对优势幅度。

Abstract: We introduce AdamX, a first-order optimizer that incorporates cosine similarity as an adaptive mechanism for controlling update magnitudes. The proposed method is scalable, model-agnostic, and straightforward to integrate into existing training pipelines. We further introduce a variance rectification scheme that promotes smoother optimization during the early stages of training. Overall, we provide empirical evidence that AdamX achieves competitive convergence rates across a range of benchmark datasets and architectures. Performance is evaluated in terms of the number of epochs required to reach predefined performance thresholds under a fixed hyperparameter budget. Code and Experiments available at: https://github.com/FranciscoCaldas/adamX.

</details>


### [182] [The Last AI Built by Humans: Toward Genuine Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11873)
*Yi Duan,Ying Liu,Zirui Tang,Haodong Chen,Jun Zhou,Yumou Liu,Bangrui Xu,Yukai Wu,Sidi Chen,Yuhan Zhou,Haoyu Wang,Xiaoyou Yu,Shaokun Han,Xuzhou Zhu,Le Zhou,Bolin Lu,Wei Zhou,Jiachen Liu,Nuozhou Fang,Jiaxin Tian,Ruoyu Chen,Yuxuan Li,Kai Zuo,Kaiyan Zhang,Jiantao Qiu,Conghui He,Guoliang Li,Bowen Zhou,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhoufutu Wen,Jihua Kang,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"递归自我改进"（Recursive Self-Improvement, RSI）的概念框架与发展路线图，并引入"Headroom-Closed Index"（HCI）指标揭示现有LLM在自我改进能力上的局限，进而从自主性维度勾勒从改进-执行自主到递归元改进的五阶段演进路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM虽能借助经验与反馈调整行为，但难以将反馈转化为持久、可累积的能力提升，即无法真正改进"未来改进的过程"本身。论文旨在系统性地界定这一问题（通过HCI指标量化），并明确RSI作为AI系统实现能力与改进机制双重进化的重要性，对科学发现、具身智能、软件工程等场景具有关键意义。

Method: 论文提出RSI概念及其发展路线图，将自我改进能力拆解为五个层级：改进-执行自主性、改进-策略自主性、经验获取自主性、环境适应自主性，直至递归元改进（recursive meta-improvement）。同时提出Headroom-Closed Index（HCI）用于揭示现有LLM的改进瓶颈。研究进一步跨场景（科学发现、具身智能、软件工程）分析RSI的差异化需求与发展速度。

Result: 论文未描述统一基准实验，而是结合多样化行业实践与初步实证证据，将RSI研究与实际系统相连接，并识别实现真正RSI的关键挑战。分析表明不同应用场景对RSI各自主性层级的需求和演进速度存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出RSI概念体系、以HCI揭示现有LLM局限、并给出五阶段自主性演进路线图，同时跨场景梳理RSI需求与挑战。作为概念性/综述性工作，其局限在于缺乏系统性定量验证与标准化评测协议，实证支持相对初步。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) enables AI systems to turn experience and feedback into persistent changes that improve both their capabilities and the process of future improvement. We first use the Headroom-Closed Index (HCI) to reveal the problems of existing LLMs, then introduce the RSI concept and its development roadmap: from improvement-execution autonomy, improvement-strategy autonomy, experience-acquisition autonomy, and environment-adaptation autonomy, to recursive meta-improvement. Next we examine RSI across scenarios (e.g., scientific discovery, embodied intelligence, software engineering), highlighting their distinct requirements and development speeds. Drawing on diverse industry practices and preliminary empirical evidence, we connect RSI research with practical systems and identify key challenges to achieving genuine RSI.

</details>


### [183] [CoRA-NAS: Coarse Ranking and Anchor-Residual Refinement for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11884)
*Yifan Yang,Zhaoyan Wang,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CoRA-NAS 两阶段零成本代理框架：第一阶段 CoRA-Rank 用等权对数秩共识聚合容量与初始化结构代理，并加无目标共识门；第二阶段 CoRA-Refine 在排序先验上采样锚点、外推早期验证曲线，并以 ExtraTrees 学习残差修正，仅用约 1% 训练成本实现跨搜索空间的鲁棒架构排序与选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零成本代理（zero-cost proxies）能以极低开销对神经网络架构排序，但其可靠性在不同搜索空间间波动很大，单一代理的先验假设往往只在特定空间成立。这导致代理排序与真实精度之间相关性不稳定，进而影响 NAS 架构选择的准确性。论文旨在构建一个跨搜索空间都保持高排序鲁棒性、同时维持低成本的排序与选择框架，且不依赖全训练精度标签来拟合排序器。

Method: 方法为两阶段：

第一阶段 CoRA-Rank（静态排序先验）：聚合多类零成本代理，包括容量类（capacity）与初始化结构类（structure-at-initialization）代理，通过 equal-weight log-rank consensus（等权对数秩共识）融合，并引入 target-free consensus gate（无目标共识门）对候选排序进行门控，不接触全训练精度标签。

第二阶段 CoRA-Refine（低成本学习曲线精修）：在静态先验上采样锚点架构，外推其早期验证曲线（learning-curve extrapolation），再用 ExtraTrees 模型学习并传播残差修正（anchor-residual correction）。该精修阶段仅消耗约 1% 的全训练成本。

框架在多个搜索空间共享同一配置，仅使用空间特定的架构编码（space-specific architecture encodings）。全训练架构-精度标签不用于拟合排序器。

Result: 在 NAS-Bench-201、NAS-Bench-101、TransNAS-Bench-101 与 NATS-SSS 四个基准上评估，使用 Spearman 秩相关系数衡量排序质量。CoRA-Refine 的平均 Spearman 相关分别为 0.946、0.715、0.786、0.894；其最差空间相关性 0.715 在所有对比方法中最高。在 NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100 上，所选架构达到 73.32% 精度，接近报告的 ground-truth 最优 73.37%。在纯 size 空间上，精修弥补了静态先验相对参数量指标的不足，同时在噪声范围内与最强容量代理持平。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出结合静态排序先验与低成本学习曲线残差精修的跨空间零成本 NAS 框架，在多个基准上取得更高的平均与最差空间排序鲁棒性，并以约 1% 训练成本选出接近最优的架构，且不依赖全训练精度标签拟合。意义在于提升零成本代理在异构搜索空间中的泛化性与可靠性。潜在局限：依赖早期验证曲线外推与 ExtraTrees 残差模型的稳定性，精修效果可能受锚点采样数量与训练预算影响；纯 size 空间上仅与最强容量代理持平而非超越。

Abstract: Zero-cost proxies rank architectures cheaply, but their reliability varies across search spaces. We introduce CoRA-NAS (COarse Ranking + Anchor-residual), a two-stage framework combining a static ranking prior with low-cost learning-curve refinement. CoRA-Rank aggregates capacity and structure-at-initialization proxies through an equal-weight log-rank consensus and a target-free consensus gate. CoRA-Refine samples anchors across this prior, extrapolates their early validation curves, and propagates a learned residual correction with an ExtraTrees model. The refinement uses approximately 1% of the cost of fully training the candidate set. Fully trained architecture-accuracy labels are not used to fit the ranker. One configuration is used across spaces, with space-specific architecture encodings. Across NAS-Bench-201, NAS-Bench-101, TransNAS-Bench-101, and NATS-SSS, CoRA-Refine achieves mean Spearman correlations of 0.946, 0.715, 0.786, and 0.894, respectively. Its worst-space correlation of 0.715 is the highest among the compared methods. On NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100, its selected architecture reaches 73.32% accuracy, near the reported ground-truth best of 73.37%. On the pure size space, refinement recovers the static prior's shortfall relative to parameter count, while remaining tied with the strongest capacity proxies within noise. The resulting framework combines cross-space ranking robustness with low-cost architecture selection.

</details>


### [184] [CausalArena: Benchmarking Causal Discovery in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11897)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CausalArena——一个统一且可演进的因果发现基准测试平台，通过合成结构因果模型（SCMs）、语义可审计的操作型 SCM、公式驱动的科学机制 SCM 以及真实世界数据集，在统一协议下评估经典、神经与预训练（基础模型）因果发现方法，揭示不同 SCM 族与协议下方法排名发生显著变化，并指出预训练—评测重叠是基础模型时代评测的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现旨在从数据中推断因果结构，对科学推理和干预决策至关重要，其评测高度依赖结构因果模型（SCM，即因果图与其数据生成机制）。然而现有研究在因果图族（graph families）、生成机制（mechanisms）与评测协议（protocols）上差异巨大，导致结果难以横向比较。因果发现基础模型（CDFMs）的出现进一步加剧了这一问题：其表现可能不仅反映因果发现能力，还可能来自预训练环境与测试 SCM 的重叠，使得在固定合成基准上的成绩难以解释，缺乏外部效度。

Method: CausalArena 以统一协议组织多类评测环境：1）合成 SCM，提供对结构与机制的可控覆盖广度；2）语义操作型 SCM（semantic operational SCM），构造人类可审计、具语义基础的场景，超越标准合成生成器；3）公式驱动 SCM（formula-grounded SCM），在明确的科学机制下检验发现能力；4）公开真实世界数据集，作为外部效度验证。该基准被设计为统一且"可演进"，支持跨经典方法、神经网络方法与预训练/基础模型方法的一致评测，并用以显式考察预训练环境与测试 SCM 的重叠。

Result: 在覆盖经典、神经及预训练方法的实验中，作者报告了在不同 SCM 族与评测协议下方法排名出现显著位移（substantial ranking shifts），表明在某一基准范式下的强表现并不能可靠迁移到其他范式。因此，单一固定合成基准上的高分可能具有误导性。结果同时凸显基准多样性与预训练—评测重叠是基础模型时代评测因果发现的两大核心挑战。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出 CausalArena 这一统一、多族、可演进的因果发现评测基准，通过引入语义化与公式化的科学机制环境及真实数据外部效度检验，暴露了既有"单一合成基准"评测的脆弱性，并将预训练—评测重叠问题提升为显式评测维度。其意义在于为 CDFMs 时代的因果发现提供更可解释、可比较的评测框架；潜在局限在于摘要未给出具体数据集规模、指标细节与可复现性说明，且新增的语义与公式驱动 SCM 的可扩展性与覆盖完备性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover causal structures from data and is fundamental to scientific reasoning and intervention-based decision making. Its evaluation relies heavily on structural causal models (SCMs), which specify a causal graph together with the mechanisms that generate data, yet existing studies differ substantially in graph families, mechanisms, and evaluation protocols. The emergence of causal discovery foundation models (CDFMs) further complicates evaluation: performance may reflect not only causal discovery ability, but also overlap between pretraining environments and test SCMs, making results on fixed synthetic benchmarks difficult to interpret. We introduce CausalArena, a unified and evolvable benchmark for causal discovery under a common protocol. Synthetic SCMs supply controlled breadth over structures and mechanisms; semantic operational SCMs provide human-auditable, semantically grounded environments beyond standard synthetic generators; and formula-grounded SCMs test discovery under explicit scientific mechanisms. Public real-world datasets provide an additional external-validity check. Experiments across classical, neural, and pretrained methods reveal substantial ranking shifts across SCM families and protocols, showing that strong performance in one benchmark regime does not reliably transfer to others. These results highlight benchmark diversity and pretraining--evaluation overlap as central challenges for evaluating causal discovery in the foundation model era.

</details>


### [185] [TART: A Modular Tool for Technique-Aware Audio-to-Tablature Guitar Transcription](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11904)
*Akshaj Gupta,Hwi Joo Park,Andrea Guzman,Shamak Gowda,Samhita Konduri,Jiachen Lian,Robin Netzorg,Gopala Anumanchipalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TART 是一个模块化四阶段"音频到吉他谱（tablature）"自动转写流水线，能直接从吉他音频生成同时包含指法（弦-品）与演奏技法标注的吉他谱，并在含噪基准上取得显著性能提升，是首个联合生成指法与技法标注的吉他谱生成框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吉他自动音乐转写（AMT）长期受限于三个问题：（1）现有系统难以捕捉滑音（slides）、推弦（bends）、打板/敲击音（percussive hits）等表现性演奏技法；（2）常将音符错误地分配到不正确的"弦-品"组合上，导致指法错误；（3）多基于干净录音训练，对真实含噪音频的泛化能力差。这三点直接影响转写结果的音乐可演奏性与实用性，因此需要一个能同时处理技法识别、指法推定与噪声鲁棒性的统一框架。

Method: TART 采用模块化四阶段流水线：（1）音频到 MIDI 的转写模型，完成音符起始/音高检测；（2）表现性技法分类器，识别滑音、推弦、敲击等演奏技法；（3）音频条件化的 T5 编码器-解码器模型，用于"弦-品"指派（fingering/string-fret assignment），将音频信息作为条件输入以辅助正确指法推断；（4）自动吉他谱生成器，将前序输出整合为可读 tablature。核心创新在于将指法指派建模为序列到序列的条件生成任务，并显式引入技法标注分支，而非仅输出音高序列。

Result: 在零样本（zero-shot）设定下，于 GuitarSet、EGDB 以及两个增强基准 Noisy GuitarSet 和 Noisy EGDB 四个数据集上进行评测。四个基准平均结果为：音频到 MIDI 的 F50 达 81.35%（较最佳先前基线提升 +6.67 点）、弦-品的 Tab F1 为 71.8%（提升 +8.5 点）、端到端 Tab F1 为 54.08%。结果表明 TART 在含噪条件下仍保持较好泛化能力，并在指法与 MIDI 转写上均超越现有基线。

Conclusion: TART 的主要贡献是提出了首个能直接从吉他音频生成带指法与表现性技法标注的吉他谱框架，通过四阶段模块化设计分别解决技法建模、指法指派与噪声鲁棒性问题，并在多个含噪基准上验证了有效性。可能的局限包括：端到端 Tab F1（54.08%）仍明显低于分阶段指标，误差会跨模块累积；评测以零样本为主，未报告在更大规模或更真实噪声分布上的表现；技法分类与弦-品指派的泛化边界（如不同演奏风格、录音设备）尚不明确。

Abstract: Automatic Music Transcription (AMT) for guitar remains limited by three challenges: existing systems often fail to capture expressive techniques such as slides, bends, and percussive hits; they often assign notes to incorrect string-fret combinations; and they are typically trained on clean recordings, limiting their generalization to noisy real-world audio. To address these challenges, we propose TART, a modular four-stage audio-to-tablature pipeline consisting of (1) an audio-to-MIDI transcription model, (2) an expressive technique classifier, (3) an audio-conditioned T5 encoder-decoder for string-fret assignment, and (4) an automated tablature generator. We evaluate TART in a zero-shot setting on GuitarSet, EGDB, and two augmented benchmarks, Noisy GuitarSet and Noisy EGDB. Averaged across these four benchmarks, TART achieves 81.35% audio-to-MIDI F50 (+6.67 points over the best prior baseline), 71.8% string-fret Tab F1 (+8.5 points over the best prior baseline), and 54.08% end-to-end Tab F1. To our knowledge, TART is the first framework to generate guitar tablature with both fingering and expressive technique annotations directly from guitar audio.

</details>


### [186] [From Protocols to Evidence: Bounded Claims for AI in Service of the Common Good](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11910)
*Nitesh V. Chawla,Paulo Benanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"制度断裂检验"（rupture test），主张负责任AI不仅要评估系统本身，还要评估其被部署时所介入的制度性失效（responsiveness、belonging、care、accountability的缺口）。论文区分了"证据约束的部署"与"测量约束的治理"，并提出RISE AI框架，用于在明确边界内对责任、包容、安全与赋权做出有据可依的有限声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的核心动机是：AI不只是制造治理难题，它同时会暴露机构在响应性、归属感、关怀与问责方面的既有失败。部署后的AI实际上是对这些制度条件的一种干预，可能修复、加剧、替代或掩盖其所遭遇的制度断裂。因此仅对系统进行技术评测是不充分的，必须同时评测系统与它所进入的制度性断裂。论文还指出，从原则到协议（EU AI Act、NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001及各类保证实践）的转变已在推进，但更困难的问题在于：这些协议究竟确立了何种约束、它们未触动何种权力结构、以及测量应在何处停止。该问题关乎AI治理的实际效力与道德正当性。

Method: 论文首先梳理现有治理协议的运作方式，即把价值承诺转化为角色、要求、记录、监督与评估。在此基础上，借助教皇利奥十四世《Magnifica Humanitas》所提供的道德框架——以人的尊严、技术权力与共同善为中心——构建"制度断裂检验"（rupture test），将制度基线（institutional baselines）与系统评估相连接。论文进一步提出两组关键区分：（1）证据约束的部署（evidence-bounded deployment），将声明限制在实际已被评估的范围之内；（2）测量约束的治理（measurement-bounded governance），记录那些即使出现有利证据也无法被推翻的约束条件。在此边界内，论文提出RISE AI架构，用于对责任（Responsibility）、包容（Inclusivity）、安全（Safety）、赋权（Empowerment）四个方面做出"有界且基于证据"的声明。该方法属于治理框架与规范分析方法，而非技术模型或算法设计。

Result: 该论文以概念分析与规范性论证为主，未报告数据集、定量指标或受控实验。其"结果"体现为分析框架的建立：识别出现有治理协议的局限（未触及的权力结构、测量止步之处）、提出rupture test以连接制度基线与系统评估、给出evidence-bounded deployment与measurement-bounded governance的区分，并构建RISE AI作为做出有界证据性声明的架构。论文论证的结论是：负责任AI需要更好的工程实践、制度层面的修复，以及持续的道德与政治判断，三者不可相互替代。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将AI伦理与治理从纯粹的系统评测扩展到"系统—制度断裂"的联合评估，并提出可操作的检验机制（rupture test）与约束性概念区分，同时给出RISE AI的声明架构，强调有界性与证据基础。其意义在于为原则到协议的转化提供更明确的边界条件，并明确指出哪些问题属于工程可测量范围、哪些必须交由制度与政治判断。局限在于：论文偏重规范性与概念性论证，缺乏实证验证、量化评估与具体部署案例；rupture test与RISE AI的操作化细节、可测量性及跨制度场景的适用性仍有待进一步明确。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence does more than create a governance problem. It can also reveal where institutions have already failed to provide responsiveness, belonging, care, and accountability. Once deployed, AI becomes an intervention in those conditions. It can repair, compound, substitute for, or conceal the failures it encounters. Responsible AI must therefore evaluate both the system and the institutional rupture into which it is introduced. The move from principles to protocols is already underway. The EU AI Act, NIST AI RMF, ISO/IEC 42001, and assurance practices translate commitments into roles, requirements, records, oversight, and assessment. The harder questions are what these protocols actually establish, whose power they leave untouched, and where measurement must stop. Pope Leo XIV's Magnifica Humanitas provides a broader moral frame centered on dignity, technological power, and the common good. Drawing on that frame, we develop a rupture test that links institutional baselines to system evaluation. We distinguish evidence-bounded deployment, which limits claims to what has actually been evaluated, from measurement-bounded governance, which records constraints that favorable evidence cannot override. Within those limits, RISE AI provides an architecture for making bounded, evidence-based claims about Responsibility, Inclusivity, Safety, and Empowerment. Responsible AI requires better engineering, institutional repair, and continued moral and political judgment.

</details>


### [187] [Data Scarcity and Model Sparsity: Mixtures-of-Experts Overfit More to Repeated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11917)
*Atindra Jha,Margaret Li,Jure Leskovec,Percy Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统研究了数据重复（data repetition）对稀疏架构（尤其是 Mixture-of-Experts, MoE）的影响，发现 MoE 在数据重复下比稠密模型退化更快，且退化程度随稀疏度增加而加剧，并分析了过拟合的内部机制及部分正则化缓解手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于高质量人类文本数据逐渐枯竭，重复使用语言模型训练数据已成为常见做法。此前关于数据重复的研究主要针对稠密激活的 Transformer，而近期成为主流的稀疏架构 MoE（计算效率更高）在数据重复下的行为尚不清楚。理解稀疏性与数据重复之间的不良交互，对于在数据受限条件下继续扩展高效大模型至关重要。

Method: 作者在不同数据重复率下训练模型，覆盖单域与多域数据混合，并系统变化 MoE 设置（专家数量、专家粒度等）。模型规模从 80M 到 1B 激活参数（总参数达 8.5B），对比稠密模型与不同稀疏度的 MoE。实验还引入现有正则化方法（如 dropout 及强掩码式正则化）作为缓解手段，并分析 MoE 内部机制（门控路由的稳定性、专家特化程度）与过拟合的关联。

Result: 结果显示 MoE 在数据重复下退化明显快于稠密模型：80M 稠密模型重复超过 8 倍数据仍退化极小，而 MoE 在 4 倍时即开始受损，并在 32 倍后反而低于稠密模型。退化程度由总参数（而非激活参数）决定，并随稀疏度上升而加剧。部分正则化方法（如 dropout）能缓解过拟合，其中强掩码式正则化可使 MoE 在数据重复超过 64 倍时仍优于稠密模型，但无任何方法能达到全唯一数据的性能。机制分析发现 MoE 路由普遍在训练早期即稳定，且专家特化与对重复数据的过拟合相关。

Conclusion: 论文首次系统揭示稀疏架构（MoE）与数据重复之间的负面交互，指出 MoE 对数据重复远比稠密模型敏感，其退化由总参数量和稀疏度主导，机制上表现为路由早期稳定与专家过度特化。研究提供了过拟合机制证据及部分缓解方案（掩码式正则化），并指出未来可通过破坏记忆模式、减少参数过度特化来改进方法。局限性在于尚未找到能完全匹配全唯一数据性能的方案。

Abstract: As the supply of human-written text is exhausted, it has become standard practice to repeat language model training data. Prior work has studied data repetition for densely activated Transformers, but the effects of data repetition remains largely unexplored for recently dominant sparse architectures such as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), despite their increased compute efficiency. We vary data repetition rates across single- and multi-domain data mixes, and across MoE settings, including expert count and granularity. We consistently find, for models ranging from 80M to 1B active (8.5B total) parameters, that MoEs degrade more rapidly under data repetition. This effect increases with sparsity, dictated by total rather than active parameters. While 80M dense models can repeat data over 8x with minimal degradation, MoEs instead begin to suffer at 4x, and deteriorate rapidly, ceding their performance benefits in all-unique data settings to underperform dense models after 32x. We experiment with existing regularization methods as a potential remedy. We find that some methods, such as dropout, can mitigate overfitting. In particular, with strong masking-based regularization, MoEs are able to outperform dense models even when data is repeated more than 64 times. However, no method fully matches the performance of all-unique training data. Finally, we analyze internal mechanisms correlated with MoE overfitting in high repetition regimes, and find that MoE routing universally stabilizes early in training, and that expert specialization correlates with overfitting to repeated data. In sum, our work addresses the adverse interactions between sparsity and data repetition: we present evidence for the core mechanisms of overfitting and its potential remediation, and suggest promising avenues for future methods to reduce over-specialization in model parameters by disrupting memorization patterns.

</details>


### [188] [General Quantification of Covariate and Concept Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11918)
*Hongbo Chen,Li Charlie Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对分布偏移下泛化理论"理想化且不可从样本估计"的问题，利用熵最优传输（entropic optimal transport）提出 $\gamma^{*}$-concept shift 这一新概念，推导出统一协变量偏移与 concept shift 的通用误差界，并给出可估计量及 DataShifts 算法，使误差界在实践中可量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布偏移（distribution shift）是机器学习泛化的核心难题，但现有学习界理论存在两个根本缺陷：一是仅适用于较窄的理想化设定（如假设源域与目标域支撑集一致），一旦源域和目标域支撑不匹配，传统 concept shift 的定义就会失效；二是这些界通常不可从有限样本估计，难以指导实际应用。因此论文希望弥合理论与实际应用之间的鸿沟，提供既能覆盖广泛设定、又能在真实场景中量化的误差界工具。

Method: 论文首先指出当源域与目标域支撑不匹配时，现有 concept shift 的定义会失效。为此，作者借助熵最优传输（entropic optimal transport）提出 $\gamma^{*}$-concept shift 这一关键概念，并由此推导出一个通用误差界，能够统一刻画 covariate shift 与 $\gamma^{*}$-concept shift。该界适用于广泛的损失函数、标签空间以及随机标注（stochastic labeling）情形，突破了以往理论的限制。进一步地，作者为这些偏移量设计了具有集中不等式保证（concentration guarantees）的估计量，并提出 DataShifts 算法，用于量化分布偏移并估计误差界，使其在多数应用中可实际计算。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数据集、实验设置或定量指标。文中主要聚焦理论贡献：推导出统一协变量偏移与 $\gamma^{*}$-concept shift 的通用误差界，并为相关偏移量提供具备集中保证的估计量，同时提出 DataShifts 算法以实现分布偏移的量化与误差界的估计。作者强调该框架适用于广泛损失函数、标签空间和随机标注，宣称是可估计的、严谨且通用的分布偏移分析工具。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于：提出 $\gamma^{*}$-concept shift 概念，解决了源域与目标域支撑不匹配时传统定义失效的问题；给出统一协变量偏移与 concept shift 的通用误差界；并提供带集中保证的估计量与 DataShifts 算法，使理论界可从样本中估计，从而连接理论与实践。其意义在于为分布偏移下的学习误差分析提供更通用、可操作的工具。局限方面：摘要未提及实证验证、具体估计误差分析或计算复杂度讨论，实际适用性与估计精度仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Generalization under distribution shift remains a core challenge in modern machine learning, yet existing learning bound theory is limited to narrow, idealized settings and is non-estimable from samples. In this paper, we bridge the gap between theory and practical applications. We first show that existing definition of concept shift breaks when the source and target supports mismatch. Leveraging entropic optimal transport, we propose a key notion: $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, and derive a general error bound unifying covariate and $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, which applies to broad loss functions, label spaces, and stochastic labeling. We further develop estimators for these shifts with concentration guarantees, and the DataShifts algorithm, which can quantify distribution shifts and estimate the error bound in most applications - a rigorous and general tool for analyzing learning error under distribution shift.

</details>
